EXECUTIVE READING

先给结论，再进入源码

核心机制

模型还没开口，用户输入已经落账；即使后面 API 或工具出错，恢复时也不会连问题本身都丢掉。

证据规模

30 条 finding / 30 处引用

主要边界

当前源码证据仍需部署级验证

适用建设

模型还没开口，用户输入已经落账；即使后面 API 或工具出错，恢复时也不会连问题本身都丢掉。

值得借鉴

  • 模型还没开口，用户输入已经落账；即使后面 API 或工具出错，恢复时也不会连问题本身都丢掉。
  • 每次再问模型前先查账：工具有没有开单不回执、旧历史是否已折叠、这一轮工具箱和提示词是否稳定。
  • 网络半路断了，不把前半句和前半个工具调用留在账上；先橡皮擦掉，再从头重放。

需要警惕

  • 当前源码证据仍需部署级验证
  • 当前源码证据仍需部署级验证
  • 当前源码证据仍需部署级验证

阅读方式

  • 先看固定提交，再看调用链
  • 事实、推断、风险分栏
  • 每条 finding 可回到源码
00 · METHOD

研究口径：不是 README 摘要

README POLICY

README 只定位入口和产品命名；实现结论必须回到运行时代码、契约或测试。

FACT POLICY

L1 运行代码优先，L2 类型/事件契约补足，L3/L4 交叉验证，L5 推断单独标记。

01 · TECHNICAL MAPS

五张逐张展开的源码证据图

架构、时序、能力、主循环和指令执行图都独占一段；节点来自源码 finding 与指令线索，点击节点可查看固定提交、行号和执行语义。

02 · COVERAGE MAP

覆盖维度与证据等级

架构与所有权verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

持久化入口、流事件状态机、重试回滚、空响应/上下文恢复与 soft interrupt。

模型与上下文verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

统一 Provider、MultiProvider 路由、认证/模型切换、provider-native tool/compaction。

模型与上下文verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

cache-aware prompt、三种压缩策略、紧急恢复、后台跨会话 memory agent。

工具注册与执行verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

typed registry、工具快照、batch、输出预算、前后台命令和 stdin。

安全、审批与权限verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

allowed/disabled tools、pre_tool hook、危险命令 gate；未发现默认 OS 容器/系统沙箱。

连接器、插件与 MCPverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

shared pool/per-session client、stdio JSON-RPC、schema late registration、按需连接。

全部指令与扩展面verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

AGENTS.md、overlay、preferred-tools、跨工具 skills、lifecycle hooks、自开发模式。

协作与子 AgentverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

轻 fan-out、持久 Swarm、deep DAG/gates/artifacts、消息/频道、崩溃回收。

观测、日志与回放verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

snapshot+JSONL、损坏恢复、事件日志/metrics/opt-out telemetry、MIT。

  172      Login {
  173          /// Provider to log in to. Equivalent to --provider for this command, e.g. `jcode login google`.
  174          // Distinct clap id: the global `--provider` flag also has id "provider";
  175          // sharing the id makes clap drop the flag inside `login` (so
  176          // `jcode login --provider x` errors) and propagate the global default
  177          // into this positional.
  178          #[arg(value_enum, id = "login_provider", value_name = "PROVIDER")]
  179          provider: Option<ProviderChoice>,

cargo

CLI 子命令 · Rust command constructor · src/cli/hot_exec.rs:315
源码 ↗

CLI 子命令；解析器先把 argv 变成结构化参数，再交给对应处理器。

cargo→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  310          get_repo_dir().ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("Could not find jcode repository"))?;
  311
  312      update::run_git_pull_ff_only(&repo_dir, true)?;
  313
  314      crate::logging::info("Building updated source version...");
  315      let build_output = ProcessCommand::new("cargo")
  316          .args(["build", "--release"])
  317          .current_dir(&repo_dir)
  318          .output()?;
  319
  320      if !build_output.status.success() {
  321          let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&build_output.stderr);
  322          let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&build_output.stdout);
  323          if !stderr.trim().is_empty() {
  324              crate::logging::error(&format!("auto-update cargo stderr:\n{}", stderr.trim()));
  325          }
  326          if !stdout.trim().is_empty() {
  327              crate::logging::info(&format!("auto-update cargo stdout:\n{}", stdout.trim()));
  328          }
  329          anyhow::bail!("cargo build failed");
  330      }
  331
  332      if let Err(e) = build::install_local_release(&repo_dir) {
  333          crate::logging::warn(&format!("auto-update install failed: {}", e));
  334      }
  335
  336      let hash = ProcessCommand::new("git")
  337          .args(["rev-parse", "--short", "HEAD"])
  338          .current_dir(&repo_dir)
  339          .output()?;
  340      let hash = String::from_utf8_lossy(&hash.stdout);
  341      let version = format!("main-{}", hash.trim());
  342      Bus::global().publish(BusEvent::UpdateStatus(UpdateStatus::Installed {
  343          version: version.clone(),
  344      }));
  345
  346      // With a live TUI session, let the app reload gracefully (input line
  347      // saved, current turn finished, session resumed) instead of exec-ing over
  348      // the running interface, which visibly resets the screen.
  349      if let Some(session_id) = crate::get_current_session() {
  350          use crate::bus::{ClientMaintenanceAction, SessionUpdateStatus};
  351          crate::logging::info(&format!(
  352              "Updated to {}. Requesting graceful session reload...",
  353              version
  354          ));
  355          Bus::global().publish(BusEvent::SessionUpdateStatus(
  356              SessionUpdateStatus::ReadyToReload {
  357                  session_id,
  358                  action: ClientMaintenanceAction::Update,
  359                  version,
  360              },
  361          ));
  362          return Ok(());
  363      }
  364
  365      crate::logging::info(&format!("Updated to {}. Restarting...", version));
  366      std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(250));
  367
  368      let exe = build::client_update_candidate(false)
  369          .map(|(p, _)| p)
  370          .or_else(|| std::env::current_exe().ok())
  371          .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("No executable path found after update"))?;
  372      let args: Vec<String> = std::env::args().skip(1).collect();
  373
  374      let err =

  623      /// Prefer `server reload` after an upgrade; it preserves live sessions.
  624      /// `stop` terminates the daemon (SIGTERM, escalating to SIGKILL), which
  625      /// drops any in-flight headless/swarm sessions, so it requires `--force`
  626      /// as a deliberate acknowledgement.
  627      Stop {

git

CLI 子命令 · Rust command constructor · crates/jcode-tui/src/tui/app/commands.rs:1490
源码 ↗

CLI 子命令；解析器先把 argv 变成结构化参数，再交给对应处理器。

git→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
 1485      std::env::current_dir()
 1486          .map_err(|_| "Unable to determine a working directory for /git.".to_string())
 1487  }
 1488
 1489  fn run_git_command(repo_dir: &std::path::Path, args: &[&str]) -> Result<String, String> {
 1490      let output = Command::new("git")
 1491          .args(args)
 1492          .current_dir(repo_dir)
 1493          .output()
 1494          .map_err(|error| format!("Failed to run git {}: {}", args.join(" "), error))?;
 1495
 1496      if !output.status.success() {
 1497          let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).trim().to_string();
 1498          let failure = if stderr.is_empty() {
 1499              format!(
 1500                  "git {} exited with status {}",
 1501                  args.join(" "),
 1502                  output.status
 1503              )
 1504          } else {
 1505              stderr
 1506          };
 1507          return Err(failure);
 1508      }
 1509
 1510      Ok(String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout)
 1511          .trim_end()
 1512          .to_string())
 1513  }
 1514
 1515  fn build_git_status_message_for_dir(repo_dir: PathBuf) -> Result<String, String> {
 1516      let repo_root =
 1517          run_git_command(&repo_dir, &["rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"]).map_err(|error| {
 1518              format!(
 1519                  "No git repository found for {}: {}",
 1520                  repo_dir.display(),
 1521                  error
 1522              )
 1523          })?;
 1524      let status = run_git_command(&repo_dir, &["status", "--short", "--branch"])?;
 1525
 1526      let repo_root_path = std::path::Path::new(&repo_root);
 1527      let relative_dir = repo_dir
 1528          .strip_prefix(repo_root_path)
 1529          .ok()
 1530          .and_then(|path| {
 1531              if path.as_os_str().is_empty() {
 1532                  None
 1533              } else {
 1534                  Some(path.display().to_string())
 1535              }
 1536          })
 1537          .unwrap_or_else(|| ".".to_string());
 1538
 1539      let heading = if relative_dir == "." {
 1540          format!("/git in {}", repo_root)
 1541      } else {
 1542          format!("/git in {} ({})", repo_root, relative_dir)
 1543      };
 1544
 1545      let status_block = status
 1546          .lines()
 1547          .map(|line| format!("  {}", line))
 1548          .collect::<Vec<_>>()
 1549          .join("\n");
打开这条指令的动态执行图 →

展开逐行源码证据
  383      define_class!(
  384             // SAFETY: NSObject has no subclassing requirements and MenuHandler
  385             // does not implement Drop.
  386             #[unsafe(super(NSObject))]
  387             #[thread_kind = MainThreadOnly]
  388             #[name = "JcodeMenubarHandler"]
  389             struct MenuHandler;
  390
  391             impl MenuHandler {
  392                 /// Open the clicked session (stored in the item's representedObject)
  393                 /// in a new terminal window via `jcode --resume <id>`.
  394                 #[unsafe(method(openSession:))]
  395                 fn open_session(&self, sender: &NSMenuItem)
  396      {
  397                     let Some(object) = sender.representedObject() else {
  398                         return;
  399                     };
  400                     let Ok(session_id) = object.downcast::<NSString>() else {
  401                         return;
  402                     };
  403                     launch_jcode_window(vec!["--resume".to_string(), session_id.to_string()]);
  404                 }
  405
  406                 /// Launch a brand-new jcode session in a new terminal window.
  407                 #[unsafe(method(newWindow:))]
  408                 fn new_window(&self, _sender: &NSMenuItem) {
  409                     launch_jcode_window(Vec::new());
  410                 }
  411             }
  412         );
  413
  414      impl MenuHandler {
  415          fn new(mtm: MainThreadMarker) -> Retained<Self> {
  416              let this = Self::alloc(mtm).set_ivars(());
  417              unsafe { msg_send![super(this), init] }
  418          }
  419      }
  420
  421      /// Launch a jcode window off the main thread so slow terminal startup
  422      /// (osascript / `open`) never blocks the menu bar UI.
  423      fn launch_jcode_window(args: Vec<String>) {
  424          std::thread::spawn(move || {
  425              if let Err(err) = crate::setup_hints::launch_jcode_in_macos_terminal(&args) {
  426                  crate::logging::warn(&format!(
  427                      "menubar: failed to launch jcode window ({args:?}): {err}"
  428                  ));
  429              }
  430          });
  431      }
  432
  433      pub(super) fn run_status_item_app() {
  434          let mtm = MainThreadMarker::new()
  435              .expect("jcode menubar must run on the main thread (the process entry point)");
  436
  437          // Defense in depth: a process running under an explicit test/temp marker
  438          // must never paint into the real user's menu bar. The real protection
  439          // against duplicates is `ensure_menubar_helper_running` (which refuses
  440          // to spawn from sandboxes) plus the global singleton lock below, but a
  441          // stray `jcode menubar` invoked directly inside a test harness should
  442          // still never realize a status item.
  443          if super::env_truthy("JCODE_TEST_SESSION") || super::env_truthy("JCODE_TEMP_SERVER") {
  444              return;
  445          }

jcode-notification-broker-delegate

CLI 子命令 · named CLI command · src/cli/macos_notification_broker.rs:42
源码 ↗

[thread_kind = MainThreadOnly]

jcode-notification-broker-delegate→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
   37      define_class!(
   38          // SAFETY: NSObject has no subclassing requirements and BrokerDelegate
   39          // has no Drop implementation or Rust ivars.
   40          #[unsafe(super = NSObject)]
   41          #[thread_kind = MainThreadOnly]
   42          #[name = "JcodeNotificationBrokerDelegate"]
   43          struct BrokerDelegate;
   44
   45          // SAFETY: NSObjectProtocol has no additional safety requirements.
   46          unsafe impl NSObjectProtocol for BrokerDelegate {}
   47
   48          // SAFETY: Method signatures exactly match UNUserNotificationCenterDelegate.
   49          unsafe impl UNUserNotificationCenterDelegate for BrokerDelegate {
   50              #[unsafe(method(userNotificationCenter:willPresentNotification:withCompletionHandler:))]
   51              fn will_present(
   52                  &self,
   53                  _center: &UNUserNotificationCenter,
   54                  _notification: &objc2_user_notifications::UNNotification,
   55                  completion_handler: &block2::DynBlock<dyn Fn(UNNotificationPresentationOptions)>,
   56              ) {
   57                  completion_handler.call((UNNotificationPresentationOptions::Banner
   58                      | UNNotificationPresentationOptions::List
   59                      | UNNotificationPresentationOptions::Sound,));
   60              }
   61
   62              #[unsafe(method(userNotificationCenter:didReceiveNotificationResponse:withCompletionHandler:))]
   63              fn did_receive_response(
   64                  &self,
   65                  _center: &UNUserNotificationCenter,
   66                  response: &UNNotificationResponse,
   67                  completion_handler: &block2::DynBlock<dyn Fn()>,
   68              ) {
   69                  let content = response.notification().request().content();
   70                  let metadata = content.userInfo();
   71                  // SAFETY: the broker creates this dictionary with NSString keys
   72                  // and values. Notification Center property-list serialization
   73                  // preserves those concrete types across process launches.
   74                  let metadata = unsafe { metadata.cast_unchecked::<NSString, NSString>() };
   75                  let key = NSString::from_str(ORIGIN_METADATA_KEY);
   76                  let route = metadata
   77                      .objectForKey(&key)
   78                      .map(|value| value.to_string())
   79                      // Read the v1 target identifier too, so notifications posted
   80                      // by a broker upgraded in place remain clickable.
   81                      .or_else(|| {
   82                          content
   83                              .targetContentIdentifier()
   84                              .map(|value| value.to_string())
   85                      })
   86                      .and_then(|value| serde_json::from_str(&value).ok());
   87                  if let Some(origin) = route {
   88                      crate::notifications::activate_macos_notification_origin(&origin);
   89                  }
   90                  completion_handler.call(());
   91              }
   92          }
   93      );
   94
   95      impl BrokerDelegate {
   96          fn new(mtm: MainThreadMarker) -> Retained<Self> {
   97              let this = Self::alloc(mtm).set_ivars(());
   98              // SAFETY: NSObject's `init` signature is correct for this subclass.
   99              unsafe { msg_send![super(this), init] }
  100          }
  101      }

  600      /// Internal: hold a lightweight connection open until stdin closes.
  601      #[command(hide = true)]
  602      Keepalive,
  603
  604      /// Gracefully reload the running background server onto the newest binary.
  605      ///
  606      /// This is the preferred way to pick up an upgrade: the daemon hands its
  607      /// live sessions off to a freshly exec'd server (the same path `/reload`
  608      /// uses), so headless/swarm work is preserved instead of being killed. If
  609      /// no server is running, this is a no-op. Use `server stop --force` only
  610      /// when you need to hard-retire a wedged daemon.
  611      Reload {
  612          /// Reload even if the running server is already on the newest binary.
  613          #[arg(long)]
  614          force: bool,
  615
  616          /// Emit JSON instead of human-readable text
  617          #[arg(long)]
  618          json: bool,
  619      },
  620
  621      /// Stop the running background server and clear its socket.
  622      ///
  623      /// Prefer `server reload` after an upgrade; it preserves live sessions.
  624      /// `stop` terminates the daemon (SIGTERM, escalating to SIGKILL), which
  625      /// drops any in-flight headless/swarm sessions, so it requires `--force`
  626      /// as a deliberate acknowledgement.
  627      Stop {
  628          /// Confirm that terminating the daemon (and dropping live sessions) is intended.
  629          #[arg(long)]
  630          force: bool,
  631
  632          /// Emit JSON instead of human-readable text
  633          #[arg(long)]
  634          json: bool,
  635      },
  636  }

  833              .collect();
  834          // Only ever the bridge this check started: the newest pid, and only
  835          // one that was not running before we began.
  836          let mine = pids.into_iter().max()?;
  837          if kill {
  838              let _ = std::process::Command::new("kill")
  839                  .arg(mine.to_string())

  844      let pre_existing = bridge(false);
  845
  846      let (updates, _outgoing) = harness::spawn(|| {});
  847      let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + std::time::Duration::from_secs(90);
  848      let mut first_session: Option<String> = None;
  849      let mut failure: Option<String> = None;
  850      let mut dropped = false;
  851
  852      while std::time::Instant::now() < deadline {
  853          let Ok(update) = updates.recv_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(1)) else {
  854              continue;
  855          };
  856          match update {
  857              harness::HarnessUpdate::Status(status) => println!("[reconnect] status: {status}"),
  858              harness::HarnessUpdate::Failed(message) => {
  859                  println!("[reconnect] failure: {message}");
  860                  failure = Some(message);
  861              }
  862              harness::HarnessUpdate::Attached { session_id, .. } => match first_session.clone() {
  863                  None => {
  864                      println!("[reconnect] attached: {session_id}");
  865                      first_session = Some(session_id);
  866                      // Drop the bridge this check started. It comes back on the
  867                      // next attempt (`ensure_runtime` starts it), so this is the
  868                      // same shape as the runtime restart a rebuild produces.
  869                      match bridge(false) {
  870                          Some(pid) if Some(pid) != pre_existing => {
  871                              bridge(true);
  872                              dropped = true;
  873                              println!("[reconnect] dropped the bridge (pid {pid})");
  874                          }
  875                          _ => anyhow::bail!(
  876                              "could not identify this check's own bridge process to drop"
  877                          ),
  878                      }
  879                  }
  880                  Some(previous) => {
  881                      if !dropped {
  882                          continue;
  883                      }
  884                      let reported = failure.clone().ok_or_else(|| {
  885                          anyhow::anyhow!("reconnected without ever reporting a failure")
  886                      })?;
  887                      if session_id != previous {
  888                          anyhow::bail!(
  889                              "reconnect landed in a different session: {previous} -> {session_id}"
  890                          );
  891                      }
  892                      println!("[reconnect] re-attached {session_id} after: {reported}");
  893                      println!("[reconnect] OK");
  894                      let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&runtime);
  895                      return Ok(());
  896                  }
  897              },

  589  }
  590
  591  #[derive(Subcommand, Debug)]
  592  pub(crate) enum ServerCommand {
  593      /// Start the background server if it is not already running.
  594      Start {
  595          /// Emit JSON instead of human-readable text
  596          #[arg(long)]
  597          json: bool,
  598      },
  599
  600      /// Internal: hold a lightweight connection open until stdin closes.
  601      #[command(hide = true)]
  602      Keepalive,
  603
  604      /// Gracefully reload the running background server onto the newest binary.
  605      ///
  606      /// This is the preferred way to pick up an upgrade: the daemon hands its
  607      /// live sessions off to a freshly exec'd server (the same path `/reload`
  608      /// uses), so headless/swarm work is preserved instead of being killed. If
  609      /// no server is running, this is a no-op. Use `server stop --force` only
  610      /// when you need to hard-retire a wedged daemon.
  611      Reload {
  612          /// Reload even if the running server is already on the newest binary.
  613          #[arg(long)]
  614          force: bool,
  615
  616          /// Emit JSON instead of human-readable text
  617          #[arg(long)]
  618          json: bool,
  619      },
  620
  621      /// Stop the running background server and clear its socket.
  622      ///
  623      /// Prefer `server reload` after an upgrade; it preserves live sessions.
  624      /// `stop` terminates the daemon (SIGTERM, escalating to SIGKILL), which
  625      /// drops any in-flight headless/swarm sessions, so it requires `--force`
  626      /// as a deliberate acknowledgement.
  627      Stop {
  628          /// Confirm that terminating the daemon (and dropping live sessions) is intended.
  629          #[arg(long)]
  630          force: bool,
  631
  632          /// Emit JSON instead of human-readable text
  633          #[arg(long)]
  634          json: bool,
  635      },
  636  }
  637
  638  #[derive(Subcommand, Debug)]
  639  pub(crate) enum CloudCommand {
  640      /// Upload, list, verify, and view cloud-synced sessions
  641      Sessions {
  642          #[command(subcommand)]
  643          action: CloudSessionsCommand,
  644      },
  645  }
  646
  647  #[derive(Subcommand, Debug)]

pgrep

CLI 子命令 · Rust command constructor · crates/jcode-desktop2/src/cli.rs:826
源码 ↗

SAFETY: single-threaded, before any connection thread is spawned.

pgrep→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  821      // SAFETY: single-threaded, before any connection thread is spawned.
  822      unsafe {
  823          std::env::set_var("JCODE_API_SOCKET", runtime.join("api.sock"));
  824      }
  825      let bridge = |kill: bool| -> Option<u32> {
  826          let listening = std::process::Command::new("pgrep")
  827              .args(["-f", "jcode-harness-api-bridge"])
  828              .output()
  829              .ok()?;
  830          let pids: Vec<u32> = String::from_utf8_lossy(&listening.stdout)
  831              .lines()
  832              .filter_map(|line| line.trim().parse().ok())
  833              .collect();
  834          // Only ever the bridge this check started: the newest pid, and only
  835          // one that was not running before we began.
  836          let mine = pids.into_iter().max()?;
  837          if kill {
  838              let _ = std::process::Command::new("kill")
  839                  .arg(mine.to_string())
  840                  .status();
  841          }
  842          Some(mine)
  843      };
  844      let pre_existing = bridge(false);
  845
  846      let (updates, _outgoing) = harness::spawn(|| {});
  847      let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + std::time::Duration::from_secs(90);
  848      let mut first_session: Option<String> = None;
  849      let mut failure: Option<String> = None;
  850      let mut dropped = false;
  851
  852      while std::time::Instant::now() < deadline {
  853          let Ok(update) = updates.recv_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(1)) else {
  854              continue;
  855          };
  856          match update {
  857              harness::HarnessUpdate::Status(status) => println!("[reconnect] status: {status}"),
  858              harness::HarnessUpdate::Failed(message) => {
  859                  println!("[reconnect] failure: {message}");
  860                  failure = Some(message);
  861              }
  862              harness::HarnessUpdate::Attached { session_id, .. } => match first_session.clone() {
  863                  None => {
  864                      println!("[reconnect] attached: {session_id}");
  865                      first_session = Some(session_id);
  866                      // Drop the bridge this check started. It comes back on the
  867                      // next attempt (`ensure_runtime` starts it), so this is the
  868                      // same shape as the runtime restart a rebuild produces.
  869                      match bridge(false) {
  870                          Some(pid) if Some(pid) != pre_existing => {
  871                              bridge(true);
  872                              dropped = true;
  873                              println!("[reconnect] dropped the bridge (pid {pid})");
  874                          }
  875                          _ => anyhow::bail!(
  876                              "could not identify this check's own bridge process to drop"
  877                          ),
  878                      }
  879                  }
  880                  Some(previous) => {
  881                      if !dropped {
  882                          continue;
  883                      }
  884                      let reported = failure.clone().ok_or_else(|| {
  885                          anyhow::anyhow!("reconnected without ever reporting a failure")
打开这条指令的动态执行图 →

reload

/ when you need to hard-retire a wedged daemon.

reload→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  606      /// This is the preferred way to pick up an upgrade: the daemon hands its
  607      /// live sessions off to a freshly exec'd server (the same path `/reload`
  608      /// uses), so headless/swarm work is preserved instead of being killed. If
  609      /// no server is running, this is a no-op. Use `server stop --force` only
  610      /// when you need to hard-retire a wedged daemon.
  611      Reload {
  612          /// Reload even if the running server is already on the newest binary.
  613          #[arg(long)]
  614          force: bool,
  615
  616          /// Emit JSON instead of human-readable text
  617          #[arg(long)]
  618          json: bool,
  619      },
  620
  621      /// Stop the running background server and clear its socket.
  622      ///
  623      /// Prefer `server reload` after an upgrade; it preserves live sessions.
  624      /// `stop` terminates the daemon (SIGTERM, escalating to SIGKILL), which
  625      /// drops any in-flight headless/swarm sessions, so it requires `--force`
  626      /// as a deliberate acknowledgement.
  627      Stop {
  628          /// Confirm that terminating the daemon (and dropping live sessions) is intended.
  629          #[arg(long)]
  630          force: bool,
  631
  632          /// Emit JSON instead of human-readable text
  633          #[arg(long)]
  634          json: bool,
  635      },
  636  }
  637

 3115      #[test]
 3116      fn handoff_waits_for_editor_before_resuming_terminal() {
 3117          let events = Rc::new(RefCell::new(Vec::new()));
 3118          let child_events = Rc::clone(&events);
 3119          let mut command = std::process::Command::new("sh");
 3120          command.args(["-c", "sleep 0.05"]);
 3121
 3122          let started = std::time::Instant::now();
 3123          let status = run_interactive_editor_with(
 3124              &mut command,
 3125              {
 3126                  let events = Rc::clone(&events);
 3127                  move || events.borrow_mut().push("suspend")
 3128              },
 3129              move || {
 3130                  assert!(
 3131                      started.elapsed() >= std::time::Duration::from_millis(40),
 3132                      "terminal resumed before the editor process exited"
 3133                  );
 3134                  child_events.borrow_mut().push("resume");
 3135              },
 3136          )
 3137          .expect("editor command should run");
 3138
 3139          assert!(status.success());
 3140          assert_eq!(&*events.borrow(), &["suspend", "resume"]);
 3141      }
 3142
 3143      #[test]
 3144      fn handoff_resumes_terminal_after_editor_failure_status() {
 3145          let resumed = Rc::new(RefCell::new(false));
 3146          let resumed_after = Rc::clone(&resumed);
 3147          let mut command = std::process::Command::new("sh");
 3148          command.args(["-c", "exit 7"]);
 3149
 3150          let status = run_interactive_editor_with(
 3151              &mut command,
 3152              || {},
 3153              move || {
 3154                  *resumed_after.borrow_mut() = true;
 3155              },
 3156          )
 3157          .expect("shell should launch");
 3158
 3159          assert_eq!(status.code(), Some(7));
 3160          assert!(*resumed.borrow());
 3161      }

  590
  591  #[derive(Subcommand, Debug)]
  592  pub(crate) enum ServerCommand {
  593      /// Start the background server if it is not already running.
  594      Start {
  595          /// Emit JSON instead of human-readable text
  596          #[arg(long)]
  597          json: bool,
  598      },
  599
  600      /// Internal: hold a lightweight connection open until stdin closes.
  601      #[command(hide = true)]
  602      Keepalive,
  603
  604      /// Gracefully reload the running background server onto the newest binary.
  605      ///
  606      /// This is the preferred way to pick up an upgrade: the daemon hands its
  607      /// live sessions off to a freshly exec'd server (the same path `/reload`
  608      /// uses), so headless/swarm work is preserved instead of being killed. If
  609      /// no server is running, this is a no-op. Use `server stop --force` only
  610      /// when you need to hard-retire a wedged daemon.
  611      Reload {
  612          /// Reload even if the running server is already on the newest binary.
  613          #[arg(long)]
  614          force: bool,
  615
  616          /// Emit JSON instead of human-readable text
  617          #[arg(long)]
  618          json: bool,
  619      },
  620
  621      /// Stop the running background server and clear its socket.
  622      ///
  623      /// Prefer `server reload` after an upgrade; it preserves live sessions.
  624      /// `stop` terminates the daemon (SIGTERM, escalating to SIGKILL), which
  625      /// drops any in-flight headless/swarm sessions, so it requires `--force`
  626      /// as a deliberate acknowledgement.
  627      Stop {
  628          /// Confirm that terminating the daemon (and dropping live sessions) is intended.
  629          #[arg(long)]
  630          force: bool,
  631
  632          /// Emit JSON instead of human-readable text
  633          #[arg(long)]
  634          json: bool,
  635      },
  636  }
  637
  638  #[derive(Subcommand, Debug)]
  639  pub(crate) enum CloudCommand {
  640      /// Upload, list, verify, and view cloud-synced sessions

  642          #[command(subcommand)]
  643          action: CloudSessionsCommand,
  644      },
  645  }

  648  pub(crate) enum CloudSessionsCommand {

  651          /// Jade Session API base URL
  652          #[arg(long)]
  653          api_base: Option<String>,

/menubar

交互 slash 指令 · command module boundary · src/cli/commands/menubar.rs:384
源码 ↗

/ and this tail.

/menubar→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  379      /// separator, Quit). Session rows are inserted between the summary header
  380      /// and this tail.
  381      const MENU_TAIL_ITEMS: isize = 4;
  382
  383      define_class!(
  384             // SAFETY: NSObject has no subclassing requirements and MenuHandler
  385             // does not implement Drop.
  386             #[unsafe(super(NSObject))]
  387             #[thread_kind = MainThreadOnly]
  388             #[name = "JcodeMenubarHandler"]
  389             struct MenuHandler;
  390
  391             impl MenuHandler {
  392                 /// Open the clicked session (stored in the item's representedObject)
  393                 /// in a new terminal window via `jcode --resume <id>`.
  394                 #[unsafe(method(openSession:))]
  395                 fn open_session(&self, sender: &NSMenuItem)
  396      {
  397                     let Some(object) = sender.representedObject() else {
  398                         return;
  399                     };
  400                     let Ok(session_id) = object.downcast::<NSString>() else {
  401                         return;
  402                     };
  403                     launch_jcode_window(vec!["--resume".to_string(), session_id.to_string()]);
  404                 }
  405
  406                 /// Launch a brand-new jcode session in a new terminal window.
  407                 #[unsafe(method(newWindow:))]
  408                 fn new_window(&self, _sender: &NSMenuItem) {
  409                     launch_jcode_window(Vec::new());
  410                 }
  411             }
  412         );
  413
  414      impl MenuHandler {
  415          fn new(mtm: MainThreadMarker) -> Retained<Self> {
  416              let this = Self::alloc(mtm).set_ivars(());
  417              unsafe { msg_send![super(this), init] }
  418          }
  419      }
  420
  421      /// Launch a jcode window off the main thread so slow terminal startup
  422      /// (osascript / `open`) never blocks the menu bar UI.
  423      fn launch_jcode_window(args: Vec<String>) {
  424          std::thread::spawn(move || {
  425              if let Err(err) = crate::setup_hints::launch_jcode_in_macos_terminal(&args) {
  426                  crate::logging::warn(&format!(
  427                      "menubar: failed to launch jcode window ({args:?}): {err}"
  428                  ));
  429              }
  430          });
  431      }
  432
  433      pub(super) fn run_status_item_app() {
  434          let mtm = MainThreadMarker::new()
  435              .expect("jcode menubar must run on the main thread (the process entry point)");
  436
  437          // Defense in depth: a process running under an explicit test/temp marker
  438          // must never paint into the real user's menu bar. The real protection
  439          // against duplicates is `ensure_menubar_helper_running` (which refuses
  440          // to spawn from sandboxes) plus the global singleton lock below, but a
  441          // stray `jcode menubar` invoked directly inside a test harness should
  442          // still never realize a status item.
  443          if super::env_truthy("JCODE_TEST_SESSION") || super::env_truthy("JCODE_TEMP_SERVER") {
打开这条指令的动态执行图 →

01
DIMENSION · ARCHITECTURE

架构与所有权

4 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factarchitecture

01 · 每个用户 turn 先写盘，再进入可恢复的流式循环

源码事实

run_once/run_once_capture 先把用户消息加入 Session 并 save，再调用 run_turn；远程流入口还先注入跨 Agent 通知、图片和本轮 system reminder，并在 turn 前后触发 observer hooks。

白话解释

模型还没开口，用户输入已经落账；即使后面 API 或工具出错，恢复时也不会连问题本身都丢掉。

对自研 Harness 的含义

耐崩溃性强，但每轮和工具结果频繁保存会增加本地 I/O，需要 journal 快路径配合。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 4–18
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 19–35
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

    4  impl Agent {
    5      /// Run a single turn with the given user message
    6      pub async fn run_once(&mut self, user_message: &str) -> Result<()> {
    7          self.add_message(
    8              Role::User,
    9              vec![ContentBlock::Text {
   10                  text: user_message.to_string(),
   11                  cache_control: None,
   12              }],
   13          );
   14          self.session.save()?;
   15          if trace_enabled() {
   16              eprintln!("[trace] session_id {}", self.session.id);
   17          }
   18          let _ = self.run_turn(true).await?;
   19          Ok(())
   20      }
   21
   22      pub async fn run_once_capture(&mut self, user_message: &str) -> Result<String> {
   23          self.add_message(
   24              Role::User,
   25              vec![ContentBlock::Text {
   26                  text: user_message.to_string(),
   27                  cache_control: None,
   28              }],
   29          );
   30          self.session.save()?;
   31          if trace_enabled() {
   32              eprintln!("[trace] session_id {}", self.session.id);
   33          }
   34          self.run_turn(false).await
   35      }
   36
   37      /// Run one conversation turn with streaming events via mpsc channel (per-client)
   38      pub async fn run_once_streaming_mpsc(
   39          &mut self,
   40          user_message: &str,
   41          images: Vec<(String, String)>,
   42          system_reminder: Option<String>,
   43          event_tx: mpsc::UnboundedSender<ServerEvent>,
   44      ) -> Result<()> {
   45          // Inject any pending notifications before the user message
   46          let alerts = self.take_alerts();
   47          if !alerts.is_empty() {
   48              let alert_text = format!(
   49                  "[NOTIFICATION]\nYou received {} notification(s) from other agents working in this codebase:\n\n{}\n\nUse the communicate tool to coordinate with other agents (prefer dm; broadcast reaches only your spawned subtree).",
   50                  alerts.len(),
   51                  alerts.join("\n\n---\n\n")
   52              );
   53              self.add_message(
   54                  Role::User,
   55                  vec![ContentBlock::Text {
   56                      text: alert_text,
   57                      cache_control: None,
   58                  }],
   59              );
   60          }
   61
   62          self.current_turn_system_reminder =
   63              system_reminder.filter(|value| !value.trim().is_empty());
   64
   65          let mut blocks: Vec<ContentBlock> = images
   66              .into_iter()
   67              .map(|(media_type, data)| ContentBlock::Image { media_type, data })
   68              .collect();
   69          blocks.push(ContentBlock::Text {
   70              text: user_message.to_string(),
   71              cache_control: None,
   72          });
   73
   74          if blocks.len() > 1 {
   75              crate::logging::info(&format!(
   76                  "Agent received message with {} image(s)",
   77                  blocks.len() - 1
   78              ));
   79          }
   80
   81          self.add_message(Role::User, blocks);
   82          crate::telemetry::record_turn();
   83          self.session.save()?;
   84          let turn_started_at = Instant::now();
   85          let start_message_index = self.message_count();
   86          self.fire_turn_start_hook("chat");
   87          let result = self.run_turn_streaming_mpsc(event_tx).await;
   88          self.current_turn_system_reminder = None;
   89          self.fire_turn_end_hook(&result, turn_started_at, start_message_index);
   90          result
   91      }
   92
   93      /// Fire the `turn_start` observer hook when a turn begins, before the model
   94      /// starts generating (and before the first `pre_tool`). This lets external
   95      /// integrations (terminal multiplexers, status bars) detect that the agent
   96      /// is actively working during the otherwise-invisible window between prompt
   97      /// submission and the first tool call. No-op (without building the payload)
   98      /// when the hook is not configured.
   99      fn fire_turn_start_hook(&self, source: &str) {
  100          if !crate::hooks::hook_configured("turn_start") {
  101              return;
  102          }
  103          let mut event = crate::hooks::HookEvent::new("turn_start")
  104              .session_id(self.session.id.clone())
  105              .field("MODEL", self.provider_model())
  106              .field("SOURCE", source.to_string());

02
L1factarchitecture

02 · 循环在每次请求前修复工具配对并重建稳定快照

源码事实

run_turn 注册 streaming/cancel guard；每圈先补齐缺失 tool outputs，再取得可能已压缩的 provider messages，锁定 tool definitions，取上一轮准备好的 memory，并构造静态/动态分离的 prompt。

白话解释

每次再问模型前先查账：工具有没有开单不回执、旧历史是否已折叠、这一轮工具箱和提示词是否稳定。

对自研 Harness 的含义

把异常历史修复、缓存稳定和请求生命周期放在一个明确关口。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 17–41
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 42–68
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   17      pub(super) async fn run_turn(&mut self, print_output: bool) -> Result<String> {
   18          self.set_log_context();
   19          crate::session_metrics::record_turn(&self.session.id);
   20          // Mark this session as actively streaming for presence UIs (e.g. the
   21          // macOS menu bar indicator). Cleared automatically on every exit path.
   22          let _streaming_guard = crate::session::StreamingGuard::new(self.session.id.clone());
   23          // Register this turn's cancel signal so session-level cancels reach
   24          // this in-flight turn even through stale control handles (issue #428).
   25          let _turn_cancel_guard = crate::turn_cancel_registry::register_active_turn(
   26              &self.session.id,
   27              self.graceful_shutdown.clone(),
   28          );
   29          let mut final_text = String::new();
   30          let trace = trace_enabled();
   31          let mut context_limit_retries = 0u32;
   32          let mut incomplete_continuations = 0u32;
   33          let mut empty_post_tool_continuations = 0u32;
   34
   35          loop {
   36              let repaired = self.repair_missing_tool_outputs();
   37              if repaired > 0 {
   38                  logging::warn(&format!(
   39                      "Recovered {} missing tool output(s) before API call",
   40                      repaired
   41                  ));
   42              }
   43              let (messages, compaction_event) = self.messages_for_provider();
   44              if let Some(event) = compaction_event {
   45                  // Reset cache tracker and tool lock on compaction since the message history changes
   46                  self.cache_tracker.reset();
   47                  self.locked_tools = None;
   48                  if print_output {
   49                      let tokens_str = event
   50                          .pre_tokens
   51                          .map(|t| format!(" ({} tokens)", t))
   52                          .unwrap_or_default();
   53                      crate::terminal_println!(
   54                          "📦 Context compacted ({}){}",
   55                          event.trigger,
   56                          tokens_str
   57                      );
   58                  }
   59              }
   60
   61              let tools = self.tool_definitions().await;
   62              let messages: std::sync::Arc<[Message]> = messages.into();
   63              // Non-blocking memory: uses pending result from last turn, spawns check for next turn
   64              let memory_pending =
   65                  self.build_memory_prompt_nonblocking_shared(std::sync::Arc::clone(&messages), None);
   66              // Use split prompt for better caching - static content cached, dynamic not
   67              let split_prompt = self.build_system_prompt_split(None);
   68              self.log_prompt_prefix_accounting(&split_prompt, &tools);
   69
   70              // Check for client-side cache violations before memory injection.
   71              // Memory is an ephemeral suffix that changes each turn; tracking it would cause
   72              // false-positive violations every turn (prior turn's memory ≠ current history prefix).
   73              self.record_client_cache_request(&messages);
   74
   75              // The request snapshot now owns everything the provider needs. Drop
   76              // the session's derived transcript copy before the network wait.
   77              self.session.release_provider_messages_cache();
   78
   79              // Inject memory as a user message at the end (preserves cache prefix)
   80              let mut messages_with_memory: Vec<Message> = messages.iter().cloned().collect();
   81              if let Some(memory) = memory_pending.as_ref() {
   82                  let memory_count = memory.count.max(1);
   83                  let age_ms = memory.computed_at.elapsed().as_millis() as u64;
   84                  crate::memory::record_injected_prompt(&memory.prompt, memory_count, age_ms);
   85                  self.record_memory_injection_in_session(memory);
   86                  logging::info(&format!(
   87                      "Memory injected as message ({} chars)",
   88                      memory.prompt.len()
   89                  ));
   90                  let (memory_msg, _persisted) = self.prepare_memory_injection_message(memory);
   91                  messages_with_memory.push(memory_msg);
   92              }
   93
   94              logging::info(&format!(
   95                  "API call starting: {} messages, {} tools",
   96                  messages_with_memory.len(),
   97                  tools.len()
   98              ));
   99              let api_start = Instant::now();
  100
  101              // Publish status for TUI to show during Task execution
  102              Bus::global().publish(BusEvent::SubagentStatus(SubagentStatus {
  103                  session_id: self.session.id.clone(),
  104                  status: "calling API".to_string(),
  105                  model: Some(self.provider.model()),
  106              }));
  107
  108              let stamped = crate::config::config()
  109                  .features
  110                  .message_timestamps
  111                  .then(|| Message::with_timestamps(&messages_with_memory));
  112              let send_messages = stamped.as_deref().unwrap_or(&messages_with_memory);
  113              let prompt_has_recent_tool_result = Self::messages_end_with_tool_result(send_messages);
  114              self.last_status_detail = None;
  115              let mut stream = match self
  116                  .provider
  117                  .complete_split(
  118                      send_messages,
  119                      &tools,
  120                      &split_prompt.static_part,
  121                      &split_prompt.dynamic_part,
  122                      self.provider_session_id.as_deref(),
  123                  )
  124                  .await
  125              {
  126                  Ok(stream) => stream,
  127                  Err(e) => {
  128                      if self.try_auto_compact_after_context_limit(&e.to_string()) {
  129                          context_limit_retries += 1;
  130                          if context_limit_retries > Self::MAX_CONTEXT_LIMIT_RETRIES {
  131                              logging::warn(
  132                                  "Context-limit compaction retry limit reached; giving up",
  133                              );
  134                              return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
  135                                  "Context limit exceeded after {} compaction retries",
  136                                  Self::MAX_CONTEXT_LIMIT_RETRIES
  137                              ));
  138                          }
  139                          continue;

   17      const BATCH_NUDGE: &str = "<system-reminder>Several tool calls have been made one at a time. If the next independent operations can run concurrently, use the batch tool instead of making more sequential calls. Keep sequential calls when one result is required to decide the next operation.</system-reminder>";
   18
   19      fn update_sequential_tool_rounds(current: u32, tool_count: usize, used_batch: bool) -> u32 {
   20          if tool_count == 1 && !used_batch {
   21              current.saturating_add(1)
   22          } else {
   23              0
   24          }
   25      }
   26
   27      fn should_inject_batch_nudge(pending: bool, batch_available: bool) -> bool {
   28          pending && batch_available
   29      }
   30
   31      pub(super) async fn run_turn(&mut self, print_output: bool) -> Result<String> {
   32          self.set_log_context();
   33          crate::session_metrics::record_turn(&self.session.id);
   34          // Mark this session as actively streaming for presence UIs (e.g. the
   35          // macOS menu bar indicator). Cleared automatically on every exit path.
   36          let _streaming_guard = crate::session::StreamingGuard::new(self.session.id.clone());
   37          // Register this turn's cancel signal so session-level cancels reach
   38          // this in-flight turn even through stale control handles (issue #428).
   39          let _turn_cancel_guard = crate::turn_cancel_registry::register_active_turn(
   40              &self.session.id,
   41              self.graceful_shutdown.clone(),
   42          );
   43          let mut final_text = String::new();
   44          let trace = trace_enabled();
   45          let mut context_limit_retries = 0u32;
   46          let mut incomplete_continuations = 0u32;
   47          let mut empty_post_tool_continuations = 0u32;
   48          let mut fable_guardrail_reconsiderations = 0u32;
   49          let mut sequential_single_tool_rounds = 0u32;
   50          let mut batch_nudge_pending = false;
   51
   52          loop {
   53              // Do not start another provider request once a cancel has been
   54              // observed; the loop is re-entered by several recovery paths
   55              // (issue #732, regression of #428).
   56              if self.is_graceful_shutdown() {
   57                  logging::info("Cancel observed at turn-loop head - not starting another request");
   58                  break;
   59              }
   60              let repaired = self.repair_missing_tool_outputs();
   61              if repaired > 0 {
   62                  logging::warn(&format!(
   63                      "Recovered {} missing tool output(s) before API call",
   64                      repaired
   65                  ));
   66              }
   67              let (messages, compaction_event) = self.messages_for_provider();
   68              if let Some(event) = compaction_event {
   69                  // Reset cache tracker and tool lock on compaction since the message history changes
   70                  self.cache_tracker.reset();
   71                  self.locked_tools = None;
   72                  if print_output {
   73                      let tokens_str = event
   74                          .pre_tokens
   75                          .map(|t| format!(" ({} tokens)", t))
   76                          .unwrap_or_default();
   77                      crate::terminal_println!(
   78                          "📦 Context compacted ({}){}",
   79                          event.trigger,
   80                          tokens_str
   81                      );
   82                  }
   83              }
   84
   85              let tools = self.tool_definitions().await;
   86              let messages: std::sync::Arc<[Message]> = messages.into();
   87              // Non-blocking memory: uses pending result from last turn, spawns check for next turn
   88              let memory_pending =
   89                  self.build_memory_prompt_nonblocking_shared(std::sync::Arc::clone(&messages), None);
   90              // Use split prompt for better caching - static content cached, dynamic not
   91              let split_prompt = self.build_system_prompt_split(None);
   92              self.log_prompt_prefix_accounting(&split_prompt, &tools);
   93
   94              // Check for client-side cache violations before memory injection.
   95              // Memory is an ephemeral suffix that changes each turn; tracking it would cause
   96              // false-positive violations every turn (prior turn's memory ≠ current history prefix).
   97              self.record_client_cache_request(&messages);
   98
   99              // The request snapshot now owns everything the provider needs. Drop
  100              // the session's derived transcript copy before the network wait.
  101              self.session.release_provider_messages_cache();
  102
  103              // Inject memory as a user message at the end (preserves cache prefix)
  104              let mut messages_with_memory: Vec<Message> = messages.iter().cloned().collect();
  105              if let Some(memory) = memory_pending.as_ref() {
  106                  let memory_count = memory.count.max(1);
  107                  let age_ms = memory.computed_at.elapsed().as_millis() as u64;
  108                  crate::memory::record_injected_prompt(&memory.prompt, memory_count, age_ms);
  109                  self.record_memory_injection_in_session(memory);
  110                  logging::info(&format!(
  111                      "Memory injected as message ({} chars)",
  112                      memory.prompt.len()
  113                  ));
  114                  let (memory_msg, _persisted) = self.prepare_memory_injection_message(memory);
  115                  messages_with_memory.push(memory_msg);
  116              }
  117              if Self::should_inject_batch_nudge(
  118                  batch_nudge_pending,
  119                  tools.iter().any(|tool| tool.name == "batch"),
  120              ) {
  121                  messages_with_memory.push(Message::user(Self::BATCH_NUDGE));
  122                  batch_nudge_pending = false;
  123                  sequential_single_tool_rounds = 0;
  124              }
  125
  126              logging::info(&format!(
  127                  "API call starting: {} messages, {} tools",
  128                  messages_with_memory.len(),
  129                  tools.len()
  130              ));
  131              let api_start = Instant::now();
  132
  133              // Publish status for TUI to show during Task execution
  134              Bus::global().publish(BusEvent::SubagentStatus(SubagentStatus {
  135                  session_id: self.session.id.clone(),
  136                  status: "calling API".to_string(),
  137                  model: Some(self.provider.model()),
  138              }));

03
L1factarchitecture

03 · 中途断流先撤销半截状态再完整重播

源码事实

Provider 发出 RetryRollback 后，Agent 清空该次尝试的文本、tool calls、SDK results、reasoning 与 native compaction；Copilot runtime 仅在已向消费者发过可见输出且错误可重试时发送该事件。

白话解释

网络半路断了，不把前半句和前半个工具调用留在账上；先橡皮擦掉，再从头重放。

对自研 Harness 的含义

显著降低重试导致的重复文本和重复副作用；普通 stdout 无法擦除，只会显示断点标记。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 455–468
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 469–484
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  486                      }
  487                      StreamEvent::RetryRollback { attempt, max } => {
  488                          // Transient transport fault mid-stream; the provider is
  489                          // replaying the request. Discard this attempt's partial
  490                          // output so the replay doesn't duplicate it in history.
  491                          logging::warn(&format!(
  492                              "Mid-stream retry rollback (attempt {}/{}): discarding partial output ({} text chars, {} tool calls)",
  493                              attempt,
  494                              max,
  495                              text_content.len(),
  496                              tool_calls.len(),
  497                          ));
  498                          if print_output && !text_content.is_empty() {
  499                              // Already-printed text can't be unprinted on a plain
  500                              // stdout stream; mark the discontinuity instead.
  501                              println!("\n[connection interrupted, retrying response from the top]");
  502                              io::stdout().flush()?;
  503                          }
  504                          text_content.clear();
  505                          tool_calls.clear();
  506                          current_tool = None;
  507                          current_tool_input.clear();
  508                          sdk_tool_results.clear();
  509                          generated_image_contexts.clear();
  510                          reasoning_content.clear();
  511                          reasoning_signature.clear();
  512                          openai_reasoning_items.clear();
  513                          openai_native_compaction = None;
  514                          saw_message_end = false;
  515                          stop_reason = None;
  516                      }
  517                      StreamEvent::MessageEnd {
  518                          stop_reason: reason,
  519                      } => {
  520                          saw_message_end = true;
  521                          if reason.is_some() {
  522                              stop_reason = reason;
  523                          }
  524                          // Don't break yet - wait for SessionId which comes after MessageEnd
  525                          // (but stream close will also end the loop for providers without SessionId)
  526                      }
  527                      StreamEvent::SessionId(sid) => {
  528                          if trace {
  529                              eprintln!("[trace] session_id {}", sid);
  530                          }
  531                          self.provider_session_id = Some(sid.clone());
  532                          self.session.provider_session_id = Some(sid);
  533                          // We've received session_id, can exit the loop now
  534                          if saw_message_end {
  535                              break;
  536                          }
  537                      }
  538                      StreamEvent::UpstreamProvider { provider } => {
  539                          // Log upstream provider for local trace output
  540                          if trace {
  541                              eprintln!("[trace] upstream_provider={}", provider);
  542                          }
  543                          self.last_upstream_provider = Some(provider);
  544                      }
  545                      StreamEvent::OpenAIReasoning {
  546                          id,
  547                          summary,
  548                          encrypted_content,
  549                          status,
  550                      } => {
  551                          if store_reasoning_content {
  552                              openai_reasoning_items.push(ContentBlock::OpenAIReasoning {
  553                                  id,
  554                                  summary,
  555                                  encrypted_content,
04
L1factarchitecture

04 · 上下文、截断回复和工具后空回复各有独立止损上限

源码事实

上下文自动压缩重试最多 5 次，不完整回复 continuation 最多 3 次，工具结果后的空白 final 最多 5 次；工具后空白会写入一条明确要求给 final answer 的 user message。

白话解释

不同故障不共用一个模糊的“最多重试 N 次”：箱子装不下、回答被截断、工具后失语分别计数。

对自研 Harness 的含义

长任务韧性好，也避免无限循环；自动补写的 user message会成为真实历史。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 5–9
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 10–15
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   56                          tokens_str
   57                      );
   58                  }
   59              }

   62              let messages: std::sync::Arc<[Message]> = messages.into();

   64              let memory_pending =
   65                  self.build_memory_prompt_nonblocking_shared(std::sync::Arc::clone(&messages), None);
   66              // Use split prompt for better caching - static content cached, dynamic not
   67              let split_prompt = self.build_system_prompt_split(None);
   68              self.log_prompt_prefix_accounting(&split_prompt, &tools);

   71              // Memory is an ephemeral suffix that changes each turn; tracking it would cause
   72              // false-positive violations every turn (prior turn's memory ≠ current history prefix).
   73              self.record_client_cache_request(&messages);

   76              // the session's derived transcript copy before the network wait.
   77              self.session.release_provider_messages_cache();

   83                  let age_ms = memory.computed_at.elapsed().as_millis() as u64;

   85                  self.record_memory_injection_in_session(memory);

   19      fn update_sequential_tool_rounds(current: u32, tool_count: usize, used_batch: bool) -> u32 {
   20          if tool_count == 1 && !used_batch {
   21              current.saturating_add(1)
   22          } else {
   23              0
   24          }
   25      }

03
DIMENSION · MODELCONTEXT

模型与上下文

9 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L2factmodelContext

01 · Provider 契约不仅抽象生成，还抽象认证、路由、transport 与原生能力

源码事实

Provider trait 提供 complete/complete_split、模型/route/effort/tier/transport 切换、认证解析、context window、fork、native tool result sender 和 native compaction；fork 明确要求独立可变状态。

白话解释

它不是只把 URL 换掉；连“谁付费、用哪条线路、能不能续上服务端会话、工具由谁执行、压缩由谁做”都在同一接口里。

对自研 Harness 的含义

多后端能力完整，但 trait 面积很大，新 Provider 的一致性测试成本高。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 76–100
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 101–126
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   97          self.complete(&dynamic_messages, tools, system_static, resume_session_id)
   98              .await

  101      /// Get the provider name.
  102      ///
  103      /// This is the stable, machine-facing identifier (e.g. `"openrouter"`,
  104      /// `"claude"`). Several surfaces key billing and routing decisions off this
  105      /// value, so it must stay constant for a given provider class even when the
  106      /// underlying runtime is a specific OpenAI-compatible profile. Use
  107      /// [`Provider::display_name`] for anything shown to the user.
  108      fn name(&self) -> &str;
  109
  110      /// Human-facing provider label for the *current runtime selection*.
  111      ///
  112      /// Defaults to [`Provider::name`]. Provider orchestrators that multiplex
  113      /// several backends behind one `name()` (notably the OpenRouter slot, which
  114      /// also serves direct OpenAI-compatible profiles such as NVIDIA NIM or
  115      /// DeepSeek) override this so the UI reflects the profile the user actually
  116      /// selected at runtime instead of a fixed aggregator label.
  117      fn display_name(&self) -> String {
  118          self.name().to_string()
  119      }

  122      fn model(&self) -> String {
  123          "unknown".to_string()

  127      /// actually use for the next request (e.g. "OAuth" or "API key"), or `None`
  128      /// when there is no meaningful OAuth-vs-API-key distinction. UI surfaces use
  129      /// this to report the auth method accurately instead of inferring it from

  132          self.active_resolved_credential()
  133              .map(ResolvedCredential::auth_method_label)
  134      }

  136      /// The credential the active provider will actually use for the next
  137      /// request, when the provider supports both OAuth and API-key auth
  138      /// (currently Anthropic and OpenAI). Returns `None` for providers with no
  139      /// OAuth-vs-API-key ambiguity.
  140      ///
  141      /// This is the authoritative, server-side answer to "subscription or
  142      /// cost-based billing?". It is computed from the provider's live credential
  143      /// mode rather than re-derived from credential probes or env strings, so
  144      /// every surface (header tag, info-widget usage, model-switch line) and
  145      /// every transport (local or remote) agrees. Remote clients receive the
  146      /// resolved value over the wire instead of guessing from a provider name.
  147      fn active_resolved_credential(&self) -> Option<ResolvedCredential> {
  148          None
  149      }
  150

  152      /// use *when the user has explicitly pinned one* (OAuth or API key), without
  153      /// resolving "auto".
  154      ///
  155      /// Unlike [`Provider::active_resolved_credential`], this never touches disk
  156      /// or env to resolve an auto/default choice: it returns `Some` only for an
  157      /// explicit in-memory pin and `None` for auto mode (or providers with no
  158      /// OAuth-vs-API-key ambiguity). UI surfaces that rebuild every frame (the

  161      fn active_explicit_credential(&self) -> Option<ResolvedCredential> {

  182      fn set_route_selection(&self, selection: &RouteSelection) -> Result<()> {

02
L1factmodelContext

02 · MultiProvider 同时容纳九类 runtime 与兼容端点 profile

源码事实

MultiProvider 持有 Claude CLI、Anthropic API、OpenAI、Copilot、Antigravity、Gemini、Cursor、Bedrock、OpenRouter，以及独立 keyed OpenAI-compatible profiles；active provider 可热切换。

白话解释

像一个总机：同一 Agent 可以接订阅 CLI、官方 API、Copilot、Gemini、Bedrock 或自定义兼容网关，并保留各自认证身份。

对自研 Harness 的含义

模型路由覆盖面极广；OpenRouter 槽位兼容多 profile，代码中有大量防止 profile 身份丢失的分支。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 328–350
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 351–374
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  328  /// MultiProvider wraps multiple providers and allows seamless model switching
  329  pub struct MultiProvider {
  330      /// Claude Code CLI provider
  331      claude: RwLock<Option<Arc<dyn Provider>>>,
  332      /// Direct Anthropic API provider (no Python dependency)
  333      anthropic: RwLock<Option<Arc<dyn Provider>>>,
  334      openai: RwLock<Option<Arc<dyn Provider>>>,
  335      /// GitHub Copilot API provider (direct API, hot-swappable after login).
  336      /// Held as `dyn Provider`: the concrete runtime lives downstream in
  337      /// `jcode-provider-copilot-runtime` and is instantiated through
  338      /// `external::instantiate_external_provider`.
  339      copilot_api: RwLock<Option<Arc<dyn Provider>>>,
  340      /// Antigravity provider (direct HTTPS, hot-swappable after login). Held as
  341      /// `dyn Provider`: the concrete runtime lives downstream in
  342      /// `jcode-provider-antigravity-runtime` and is instantiated through
  343      /// `external::instantiate_external_provider`.
  344      antigravity: RwLock<Option<Arc<dyn Provider>>>,
  345      /// Gemini provider (hot-swappable after login). Held as `dyn Provider`:
  346      /// the concrete runtime lives downstream in `jcode-provider-gemini-runtime`
  347      /// and is instantiated through `external::instantiate_external_provider`.
  348      gemini: RwLock<Option<Arc<dyn Provider>>>,
  349      /// Cursor provider (native/direct API, hot-swappable after login). Held as
  350      /// `dyn Provider`: the concrete runtime lives downstream in
  351      /// `jcode-provider-cursor-runtime` and is instantiated through
  352      /// `external::instantiate_external_provider`.
  353      cursor: RwLock<Option<Arc<dyn Provider>>>,
  354      /// AWS Bedrock provider (native Converse/ConverseStream, IAM/SigV4)
  355      bedrock: RwLock<Option<Arc<bedrock::BedrockProvider>>>,
  356      /// OpenRouter API provider
  357      openrouter: RwLock<Option<Arc<dyn Provider>>>,
  358      /// Direct OpenAI-compatible runtimes keyed by profile id.
  359      ///
  360      /// These use the same wire protocol implementation as OpenRouter, but must
  361      /// not occupy the real OpenRouter slot. Keeping them separate prevents a
  362      /// compatible endpoint selection from corrupting later OpenRouter model
  363      /// switches, catalog display, or auth refresh handling.
  364      openai_compatible_profiles: RwLock<HashMap<String, Arc<dyn Provider>>>,
  365      active_openai_compatible_profile: RwLock<Option<String>>,
  366      active: RwLock<ActiveProvider>,
  367      /// Use Claude CLI instead of direct API (legacy mode)
  368      use_claude_cli: bool,
  369      /// Notifications generated during provider/account auto-selection.
  370      /// The TUI should drain and display these on session start.
  371      startup_notices: RwLock<Vec<String>>,
  372      /// CLI/environment selection to use when creating fresh sessions. This is
  373      /// only an initial preference and never restricts later model switches.
  374      initial_provider: Option<ActiveProvider>,
  375      /// Short-TTL memo for the full route-catalog build.
  376      ///
  377      /// Building the catalog is expensive (per-route pricing lookups, endpoint
  378      /// cache reads, credential probes) and the shared server rebuilds it for
  379      /// every connection whenever a `ModelsUpdated` bus event fans out. During
  380      /// a burst of client spawns that multiplied into hundreds of builds within
  381      /// a couple of seconds, saturating every core. The memo collapses those
  382      /// into one build per TTL window; auth/model changes invalidate it
  383      /// explicitly so pickers never see stale routes after a switch.
  384      routes_memo: Mutex<Option<RoutesMemoEntry>>,
  385      /// Number of model-catalog prefetches launched by the latest auth refresh.
  386      /// Shared by forks so the server can wait for real work rather than sleeping
  387      /// through a fixed quiet period after login.
  388      post_auth_refreshes_pending: Arc<std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize>,
  389  }
  390
  391  /// Memoized route catalog with the inputs that decide its freshness: build
  392  /// time (short TTL), the auth generation at build time (bumped by
  393  /// `AuthStatus::invalidate_cache()` on login/logout/credential edits), and the
  394  /// catalog generation (bumped by prefetch/refresh completions).
  395  #[derive(Clone)]
  396  struct RoutesMemoEntry {
  397      built_at: std::time::Instant,
  398      auth_generation: u64,
  399      catalog_generation: u64,
  400      routes: Vec<ModelRoute>,
  401      /// `listable_model_names_from_routes(&routes)`, cached because the
  402      /// non-chat-model heuristic string-scans every route name and callers
  403      /// (catalog snapshots) ask for names and routes together.
  404      listable_models: Vec<String>,
  405  }
  406
  407  /// Process-wide route-catalog memo shared across `MultiProvider` instances.
  408  ///
  409  /// The shared server forks one `MultiProvider` per client connection, so a
  410  /// per-instance memo cannot deduplicate the builds triggered by a burst of
  411  /// simultaneous client spawns: every fresh fork still built its own catalog.
  412  /// Catalog content is derived almost entirely from process-global state
  413  /// (credential files, disk caches, config), so identical forks can share one
  414  /// build. Instance-specific inputs (active provider/model/profile) are folded
  415  /// into the memo key; anything not captured is bounded by the short TTL and
  416  /// the auth/catalog generations.
  417  static GLOBAL_ROUTES_MEMO: LazyLock<Mutex<HashMap<String, RoutesMemoEntry>>> =
  418      LazyLock::new(|| Mutex::new(HashMap::new()));
  419
  420  /// Single-flight guard for catalog builds. During a client connect burst every
  421  /// connection calls `model_routes()` at nearly the same instant; without this
  422  /// they all miss the still-empty memo and build the same catalog in parallel
  423  /// (a thundering herd that pegs every core). Holding this lock across the
  424  /// build makes followers block (sleep, not spin) until the leader publishes
  425  /// its result, which they then serve from the shared memo.
  426  static GLOBAL_ROUTES_BUILD_LOCK: Mutex<()> = Mutex::new(());
  427
  428  /// Bumped whenever provider catalogs change out-of-band (prefetch completion,
  429  /// forced catalog refresh, auth changes). Invalidates every shared memo entry.
  430  static CATALOG_GENERATION: std::sync::atomic::AtomicU64 = std::sync::atomic::AtomicU64::new(0);
  431
  432  fn catalog_generation() -> u64 {
  433      CATALOG_GENERATION.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed)
  434  }
  435
  436  /// Invalidate every memoized route catalog in the process.
  437  ///
  438  /// Called when provider catalogs change out-of-band (background catalog
  439  /// refresh completion, auth changes). This is what keeps the route memo TTL a
  440  /// backstop rather than the mechanism: a completed refresh is reflected on the
  441  /// next render instead of waiting for the memo to expire.
  442  pub fn bump_catalog_generation() {
  443      CATALOG_GENERATION.fetch_add(1, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);
  444  }
  445

03
L1factmodelContext

03 · 服务端会话、原生工具和原生压缩都能穿过统一事件流

源码事实

StreamEvent::SessionId 会保存 provider resumable session；Compaction 可保存 OpenAI encrypted content；NativeToolCall 经本地 registry 执行后通过 provider native_result_sender 回传 SDK bridge。

白话解释

底层 Provider 可以说“以后用这个会话号续聊”“我已经压缩过了”或“替我本地跑这个工具”，上层都能接住。

对自研 Harness 的含义

兼容 provider-native agent runtime，而非强迫所有后端退化成纯文本 function calling。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 485–515
    入口、配置和状态初始化

  2. 516–547
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  552                      } => {
  553                          // Execute native tool and send result back to SDK bridge
  554                          if trace {
  555                              eprintln!(
  556                                  "[trace] native_tool_call request_id={} tool={}",
  557                                  request_id, tool_name
  558                              );
  559                          }
  560                          let ctx = ToolContext {
  561                              session_id: self.session.id.clone(),
  562                              message_id: self.session.id.clone(),
  563                              tool_call_id: request_id.clone(),
  564                              working_dir: self.working_dir().map(PathBuf::from),
  565                              stdin_request_tx: self.stdin_request_tx.clone(),
  566                              graceful_shutdown_signal: Some(self.graceful_shutdown.clone()),
  567                              execution_mode: ToolExecutionMode::AgentTurn,
  568                          };
  569                          crate::telemetry::record_tool_call();
  570                          let tool_result = self
  571                              .registry
  572                              .execute(&tool_name, ToolCall::normalize_input_to_object(input), ctx)
  573                              .await;
  574                          if tool_result.is_err() {
  575                              crate::telemetry::record_tool_failure();
  576                          }
  577                          let native_result = match tool_result {
  578                              Ok(output) => NativeToolResult::success(request_id, output.output),
  579                              Err(e) => NativeToolResult::error(request_id, e.to_string()),
  580                          };
  581                          // Send result back to SDK bridge
  582                          if let Some(sender) = self.provider.native_result_sender() {
  583                              let _ = sender.send(native_result).await;
  584                          }
  585                      }
  586                      StreamEvent::Error {
  587                          message,
  588                          retry_after_secs,
  589                      } => {
  590                          if trace {
  591                              eprintln!("[trace] stream_error {}", message);
  592                          }
  593                          if self.try_auto_compact_after_context_limit(&message) {
  594                              log_agent_provider_stream_lifecycle(
  595                                  logging::LogLevel::Warn,
  596                                  self,
  597                                  "stream_event_retry_after_compaction",
  598                                  api_start,
  599                                  vec![
  600                                      ("mode", "blocking".to_string()),
  601                                      ("error", message.clone()),
  602                                      (
  603                                          "context_limit_retries",
  604                                          (context_limit_retries + 1).to_string(),
  605                                      ),
  606                                  ],
  607                              );
  608                              context_limit_retries += 1;
  609                              if context_limit_retries > Self::MAX_CONTEXT_LIMIT_RETRIES {
  610                                  logging::warn(
  611                                      "Context-limit compaction retry limit reached; giving up",
  612                                  );
  613                                  return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
  614                                      "Context limit exceeded after {} compaction retries",
  615                                      Self::MAX_CONTEXT_LIMIT_RETRIES
  616                                  ));
  617                              }
  618                              retry_after_compaction = true;

  485                          self.last_status_detail = Some(detail);
