  487                      StreamEvent::RetryRollback { attempt, max } => {
  488                          // Transient transport fault mid-stream; the provider is
  489                          // replaying the request. Discard this attempt's partial
  490                          // output so the replay doesn't duplicate it in history.
  491                          logging::warn(&format!(
  492                              "Mid-stream retry rollback (attempt {}/{}): discarding partial output ({} text chars, {} tool calls)",
  493                              attempt,
  494                              max,
  495                              text_content.len(),
  496                              tool_calls.len(),
  497                          ));
  498                          if print_output && !text_content.is_empty() {
  499                              // Already-printed text can't be unprinted on a plain
  500                              // stdout stream; mark the discontinuity instead.
  501                              println!("\n[connection interrupted, retrying response from the top]");
  502                              io::stdout().flush()?;
  503                          }
  504                          text_content.clear();
  505                          tool_calls.clear();
  506                          current_tool = None;
  507                          current_tool_input.clear();
  508                          sdk_tool_results.clear();
  509                          generated_image_contexts.clear();
  510                          reasoning_content.clear();
  511                          reasoning_signature.clear();
  512                          openai_reasoning_items.clear();
  513                          openai_native_compaction = None;
  514                          saw_message_end = false;
  515                          stop_reason = None;
  516                      }
  517                      StreamEvent::MessageEnd {
  518                          stop_reason: reason,
  519                      } => {
  520                          saw_message_end = true;
  521                          if reason.is_some() {
  522                              stop_reason = reason;
  523                          }
  524                          // Don't break yet - wait for SessionId which comes after MessageEnd
  525                          // (but stream close will also end the loop for providers without SessionId)
  526                      }

  529                              eprintln!("[trace] session_id {}", sid);
  530                          }
  531                          self.provider_session_id = Some(sid.clone());
  532                          self.session.provider_session_id = Some(sid);
  533                          // We've received session_id, can exit the loop now
  534                          if saw_message_end {
  535                              break;
  536                          }
  537                      }
  538                      StreamEvent::UpstreamProvider { provider } => {
  539                          // Log upstream provider for local trace output
  540                          if trace {
  541                              eprintln!("[trace] upstream_provider={}", provider);
  542                          }
  543                          self.last_upstream_provider = Some(provider);
  544                      }
  545                      StreamEvent::OpenAIReasoning {
  546                          id,
  547                          summary,
  548                          encrypted_content,
  549                          status,
  550                      } => {
  551                          if store_reasoning_content {
  552                              openai_reasoning_items.push(ContentBlock::OpenAIReasoning {
  553                                  id,
  554                                  summary,
  555                                  encrypted_content,
  556                                  status,
  557                              });
  558                          }
  559                      }
  560                      StreamEvent::Compaction {
  561                          trigger,
  562                          pre_tokens,
  563                          openai_encrypted_content,
  564                      } => {
  565                          if let Some(encrypted_content) = openai_encrypted_content {
  566                              openai_native_compaction
  567                                  .get_or_insert((encrypted_content, self.session.messages.len()));
  568                          }
  569                          if print_output {
  570                              let tokens_str = pre_tokens
  571                                  .map(|t| format!(" ({} tokens)", t))
  572                                  .unwrap_or_default();
  573                              crate::terminal_println!(
  574                                  "📦 Context compacted ({}){}",
  575                                  trigger,
  576                                  tokens_str
  577                              );
  578                          }
  579                      }
  580                      StreamEvent::NativeToolCall {
  581                          request_id,
  582                          tool_name,
  583                          input,
  584                      } => {
  585                          // Execute native tool and send result back to SDK bridge
  586                          if trace {
  587                              eprintln!(
  588                                  "[trace] native_tool_call request_id={} tool={}",
  589                                  request_id, tool_name
  590                              );
  591                          }
  592                          let ctx = ToolContext {
  593                              session_id: self.session.id.clone(),
  594                              message_id: self.session.id.clone(),
  595                              tool_call_id: request_id.clone(),
  596                              working_dir: self.working_dir().map(PathBuf::from),
  597                              stdin_request_tx: self.stdin_request_tx.clone(),
  598                              graceful_shutdown_signal: Some(self.graceful_shutdown.clone()),
  599                              execution_mode: ToolExecutionMode::AgentTurn,
  600                          };
  601                          crate::telemetry::record_tool_call();
  602                          let tool_result = self
  603                              .registry
  604                              .execute(&tool_name, ToolCall::normalize_input_to_object(input), ctx)
  605                              .await;
  606                          if tool_result.is_err() {
  607                              crate::telemetry::record_tool_failure();
  608                          }
  609                          let native_result = match tool_result {
  610                              Ok(output) => NativeToolResult::success(request_id, output.output),
  611                              Err(e) => NativeToolResult::error(request_id, e.to_string()),
  612                          };
  613                          // Send result back to SDK bridge
  614                          if let Some(sender) = self.provider.native_result_sender() {
  615                              let _ = sender.send(native_result).await;
  616                          }
  617                      }
  618                      StreamEvent::Error {
04
L1factmodelContext

04 · 静态前缀与每轮动态上下文分离，memory 放尾部保缓存

源码事实

system prompt 把 base prompt、AGENTS、overlay、preferred-tools、skills 放 static_part；memory、active skill、reminder 与 swarm effort 放 dynamic_part。run_turn 在 client cache 检查后把 memory 作为末尾 user message注入。

白话解释

不常变的说明书放书脊，记忆和本轮提醒贴在最后一页；这样改便签不会让整本书的缓存失效。

对自研 Harness 的含义

明显以 prompt-cache economics 为一等设计目标。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 451–503
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 504–557
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  451  /// Build system prompt split into static (cacheable) and dynamic parts
  452  /// This improves cache hit rate by keeping frequently-changing content separate
  453  pub fn build_system_prompt_split(
  454      skill_prompt: Option<&str>,
  455      available_skills: &[SkillInfo],
  456      is_selfdev: bool,
  457      memory_prompt: Option<&str>,
  458      working_dir: Option<&Path>,
  459  ) -> (SplitSystemPrompt, ContextInfo) {
  460      build_system_prompt_split_with_capabilities(
  461          skill_prompt,
  462          available_skills,
  463          is_selfdev,
  464          memory_prompt,
  465          working_dir,
  466          PromptCapabilities::current(),
  467      )
  468  }
  469
  470  pub fn build_system_prompt_split_with_capabilities(
  471      skill_prompt: Option<&str>,
  472      available_skills: &[SkillInfo],
  473      is_selfdev: bool,
  474      memory_prompt: Option<&str>,
  475      working_dir: Option<&Path>,
  476      capabilities: PromptCapabilities,
  477  ) -> (SplitSystemPrompt, ContextInfo) {
  478      let mut static_parts = base_system_prompt_parts(capabilities);
  479      let mut dynamic_parts = Vec::new();
  480      let mut info = ContextInfo {
  481          system_prompt_chars: static_parts.join("\n\n").len(),
  482          ..Default::default()
  483      };
  484
  485      // === STATIC CONTENT (cacheable) ===
  486
  487      // Add self-dev guidance only in active self-dev sessions. Normal sessions
  488      // learn about the on-ramp from the mode-aware `selfdev` tool schema.
  489      if is_selfdev {
  490          let selfdev_prompt = build_selfdev_prompt_static_for_working_dir(working_dir);
  491          info.selfdev_chars = selfdev_prompt.len();
  492          static_parts.push(selfdev_prompt);
  493      }
  494
  495      // Add AGENTS.md instructions (static per project)
  496      let (md_content, md_info) = load_agents_md_files_from_dir(working_dir);
  497      if let Some(content) = md_content {
  498          static_parts.push(content);
  499      }
  500      info.has_project_agents_md = md_info.has_project_agents_md;
  501      info.project_agents_md_chars = md_info.project_agents_md_chars;
  502      info.has_global_agents_md = md_info.has_global_agents_md;
  503      info.global_agents_md_chars = md_info.global_agents_md_chars;
  504
  505      // Add optional prompt overlays from ~/.jcode/ and ./.jcode/
  506      let (overlay_content, overlay_chars) = load_prompt_overlay_files_from_dir(working_dir);
  507      if let Some(content) = overlay_content {
  508          info.prompt_overlay_chars = overlay_chars;
  509          static_parts.push(content);
  510      }
  511
  512      // Add optional preferred-tool guidance (static per project/user)
  513      let (preferred_tools_content, preferred_tools_chars) =
  514          load_preferred_tools_files_from_dir(working_dir);
  515      if let Some(content) = preferred_tools_content {
  516          info.preferred_tools_chars = preferred_tools_chars;
  517          static_parts.push(content);
  518      }
  519
  520      // Add available skills list (fairly static)
  521      if !available_skills.is_empty() {
  522          let mut skills_section = "# Available Skills\n\nYou have access to the following skills that the user can invoke with `/skillname`:\n".to_string();
  523          for skill in available_skills {
  524              skills_section.push_str(&format!("\n- `/{} ` - {}", skill.name, skill.description));
  525          }
  526          skills_section.push_str(
  527              "\n\nWhen a user asks about available skills or capabilities, mention these skills.",
  528          );
  529          info.skills_chars = skills_section.len();
  530          static_parts.push(skills_section);
  531      }
  532
  533      // === TURN CONTEXT (not cached) ===
  534
  535      // Memory prompt (changes per conversation)
  536      if let Some(memory) = memory_prompt {
  537          info.memory_chars = memory.len();
  538          dynamic_parts.push(memory.to_string());
  539      }
  540
  541      // Active skill prompt (changes per skill invocation)
  542      if let Some(skill) = skill_prompt {
  543          dynamic_parts.push(format!("# Active Skill\n\n{}", skill));
  544      }
  545
  546      let static_part = static_parts.join("\n\n");
  547      let dynamic_part = dynamic_parts.join("\n\n");
  548      info.total_chars = static_part.len() + dynamic_part.len();
  549
  550      (
  551          SplitSystemPrompt {
  552              static_part,
  553              dynamic_part,
  554          },
  555          info,
  556      )
  557  }

05
L1factmodelContext

05 · 压缩器不复制历史，只记录被摘要的前缀游标

源码事实

CompactionManager 不拥有 messages，只存 compacted_count、active_summary、active suffix char estimate 与 pending background task；恢复旧会话时先抑制压缩，直到真正的新消息加入。

白话解释

原始账本仍归 Session 管，压缩器只记“前 N 条已折叠成摘要”，不会偷偷维护第二份容易漂移的历史。

对自研 Harness 的含义

所有权清楚且便于 rewind；游标、摘要和 session persistence 必须原子同步。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 128–165
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 166–205
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

crates/jcode-base/src/compaction.rs:128–205固定提交 ↗
  128  /// Manages background compaction of conversation context.
  129  ///
  130  /// Does NOT own message data. The caller owns the messages and passes
  131  /// references into methods that need them. After compaction, the manager
  132  /// records `compacted_count` — the number of leading messages that have
  133  /// been summarized and should be skipped when building API payloads.
  134  pub struct CompactionManager {
  135      /// Number of leading messages that have been compacted into the summary.
  136      /// When building API messages, skip the first `compacted_count` messages.
  137      compacted_count: usize,
  138
  139      /// Active summary (if we've compacted before)
  140      active_summary: Option<Summary>,
  141
  142      /// Rolling char estimate for the active (non-compacted) message suffix.
  143      ///
  144      /// In the common append-only case this is maintained incrementally, so token
  145      /// estimation does not need to rescan the entire active history every time.
  146      /// Bundled with its own dirty flag so the value and staleness can never
  147      /// drift apart (see [`ActiveCharEstimate`]).
  148      active_chars: ActiveCharEstimate,
  149
  150      /// Background compaction task handle
  151      pending_task: Option<JoinHandle<Result<CompactionResult>>>,
  152
  153      /// User-facing trigger label for the currently running background compaction.
  154      pending_trigger: Option<String>,
  155
  156      /// Turn index (relative to uncompacted messages) where pending compaction will cut off
  157      pending_cutoff: usize,
  158
  159      /// Total turns seen (for tracking)
  160      total_turns: usize,
  161
  162      /// When true, session restore/reseed has just loaded old history and
  163      /// compaction must stay disabled until a genuinely new message is added.
  164      suppress_compaction_until_new_message: bool,
  165
  166      /// Token budget
  167      token_budget: usize,
  168
  169      /// Provider-reported input token usage from the latest request.
  170      /// Used to trigger compaction with real token counts instead of only heuristics.
  171      observed_input_tokens: Option<u64>,
  172
  173      /// Last compaction event (if any)
  174      last_compaction: Option<CompactionEvent>,
  175
  176      // ── Mode & strategy ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  177      /// Active compaction mode (set from config at construction)
  178      mode: crate::config::CompactionMode,
  179
  180      /// Config snapshot for mode-specific parameters
  181      compaction_config: crate::config::CompactionConfig,
  182
  183      // ── Proactive mode state ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  184      /// Rolling window of observed token counts, one entry per turn snapshot.
  185      /// Used to compute EWMA growth rate for proactive compaction.
  186      token_history: VecDeque<u64>,
  187
  188      /// Total turns elapsed since the last successful compaction.
  189      /// Used as a cooldown anti-signal.
  190      turns_since_last_compact: usize,
  191
  192      // ── Semantic mode state ────────────────────────────────────────────────
  193      /// Per-turn embedding snapshots for topic-shift detection.
  194      /// Each entry is the L2-normalized embedding of the last assistant message
  195      /// of that turn (truncated to EMBED_MAX_CHARS_PER_MSG for speed).
  196      embedding_history: VecDeque<Vec<f32>>,
  197
  198      /// Local cache for semantic compaction embeddings keyed by truncated-text hash.
  199      /// Stores both successful embeddings and failed lookups (`None`) so repeated
  200      /// semantic scans do not redo the same work.
  201      semantic_embed_cache: HashMap<u64, (Option<Vec<f32>>, u64)>,
  202
  203      /// Monotonic recency counter for the semantic embedding cache LRU.
  204      semantic_embed_cache_counter: u64,
  205  }
  206
  207  impl CompactionManager {
  208      pub fn new() -> Self {
  209          let cfg = crate::config::config().compaction.clone();
  210          let mode = cfg.mode.clone();
  211          Self {
  212              compacted_count: 0,
  213              active_summary: None,
  214              active_chars: ActiveCharEstimate::default(),
  215              pending_task: None,
  216              pending_trigger: None,
  217              pending_cutoff: 0,
  218              total_turns: 0,
  219              suppress_compaction_until_new_message: false,
  220              token_budget: DEFAULT_TOKEN_BUDGET,
  221              observed_input_tokens: None,
  222              last_compaction: None,
  223              mode,
  224              compaction_config: cfg,
  225              token_history: VecDeque::with_capacity(TOKEN_HISTORY_WINDOW + 1),
  226              turns_since_last_compact: 0,
  227              embedding_history: VecDeque::with_capacity(EMBEDDING_HISTORY_WINDOW + 1),
  228              semantic_embed_cache: HashMap::with_capacity(SEMANTIC_EMBED_CACHE_CAPACITY),
  229              semantic_embed_cache_counter: 0,
  230          }
  231      }
  232
  233      /// Reset all compaction state
  234      pub fn reset(&mut self) {
  235          *self = Self::new();
  236      }
  237
  238      pub fn with_budget(mut self, budget: usize) -> Self {
  239          self.token_budget = budget;
  240          self
  241      }
  242
  243      /// Update the token budget (e.g., when model changes)
  244      pub fn set_budget(&mut self, budget: usize) {
  245          self.token_budget = budget;
  246      }
  247
  248      /// Get current token budget
  249      pub fn token_budget(&self) -> usize {
  250          self.token_budget
  251      }
  252
  253      /// Notify the manager that a message was added.
  254      ///
  255      /// Legacy callers that do not provide the message content keep turn counts
  256      /// correct, but mark the rolling char estimate dirty so the next token
  257      /// estimate will resync from the provided history slice.
  258      pub fn notify_message_added(&mut self) {
  259          self.total_turns += 1;
  260          self.suppress_compaction_until_new_message = false;
  261          self.active_chars.invalidate();
  262      }
  263
  264      /// Notify the manager that a message was added and update the rolling char
  265      /// estimate incrementally.
  266      pub fn notify_message_added_with(&mut self, message: &Message) {
  267          self.notify_message_added_blocks(&message.content);
  268      }
  269
  270      pub fn notify_message_added_blocks(&mut self, content: &[ContentBlock]) {
  271          self.total_turns += 1;
  272          self.suppress_compaction_until_new_message = false;
  273          self.active_chars.append_exact(content_char_count(content));
  274      }

06
L1factmodelContext

06 · 压缩支持 reactive、趋势预测和语义换题三种策略

源码事实

reactive 在固定阈值触发；proactive 用 token history 的 EWMA 增长投影；semantic 用 turn embeddings 检测 topic shift，无 embedding 时回退 proactive。统一 anti-signals 防止重复、过早、停滞期和 cooldown 内压缩。

白话解释

可以等箱子快满再整理，也可以看增长速度提前整理，还可以在话题换挡时整理；但都会先检查“现在整理是否值得”。

对自研 Harness 的含义

上下文策略比多数单摘要 Agent 丰富，语义模式依赖本地 embedding 可用性和阈值调优。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 456–498
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 499–543
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

crates/jcode-base/src/compaction.rs:456–543固定提交 ↗
  456      // ── Anti-signal guard (shared by proactive + semantic) ──────────────────
  457
  458      /// Returns `true` when any anti-signal fires and we should NOT compact
  459      /// proactively right now.
  460      ///
  461      /// Anti-signals are universal guards applied before the mode-specific
  462      /// trigger logic. They prevent wasted work and respect user intent.
  463      fn anti_signals_block(&self, all_messages: &[Message]) -> bool {
  464          let cfg = &self.compaction_config;
  465
  466          // 1. Already compacting — never double-trigger.
  467          if self.pending_task.is_some() {
  468              return true;
  469          }
  470
  471          // 2. Context below the proactive floor — too early regardless of trend.
  472          let usage = self.context_usage_with(all_messages);
  473          if usage < cfg.proactive_floor {
  474              return true;
  475          }
  476
  477          // 3. Not enough token history to project from.
  478          if self.token_history.len() < cfg.min_samples {
  479              return true;
  480          }
  481
  482          // 4. Growth has stalled: last stall_window snapshots show no increase.
  483          //    If tokens haven't grown, there's no urgency.
  484          if self.token_history.len() >= cfg.stall_window {
  485              let recent: Vec<u64> = self
  486                  .token_history
  487                  .iter()
  488                  .rev()
  489                  .take(cfg.stall_window)
  490                  .cloned()
  491                  .collect();
  492              let oldest = recent[recent.len() - 1];
  493              let newest = recent[0];
  494              if newest <= oldest {
  495                  return true;
  496              }
  497          }
  498
  499          // 5. Cooldown: too soon after the last compaction.
  500          if self.turns_since_last_compact < cfg.min_turns_between_compactions {
  501              return true;
  502          }
  503
  504          false
  505      }
  506
  507      // ── Proactive mode trigger ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  508
  509      /// Returns `true` if the proactive strategy thinks we should compact now.
  510      ///
  511      /// Uses an EWMA over the token history to project forward `lookahead_turns`
  512      /// turns. If the projected token count would exceed the 80% threshold,
  513      /// it's time to compact before we get there.
  514      fn should_compact_proactively(&self, all_messages: &[Message]) -> bool {
  515          if self.anti_signals_block(all_messages) {
  516              return false;
  517          }
  518
  519          let cfg = &self.compaction_config;
  520          let budget = self.token_budget as f64;
  521          let threshold = COMPACTION_THRESHOLD as f64 * budget;
  522
  523          // Compute EWMA of per-turn token deltas.
  524          // We need at least 2 snapshots to get a delta.
  525          let snapshots: Vec<u64> = self.token_history.iter().cloned().collect();
  526          if snapshots.len() < 2 {
  527              return false;
  528          }
  529
  530          let alpha = cfg.ewma_alpha as f64;
  531          let mut ewma_delta: f64 = (snapshots[1] as f64) - (snapshots[0] as f64);
  532          ewma_delta = ewma_delta.max(0.0);
  533          for i in 2..snapshots.len() {
  534              let delta = ((snapshots[i] as f64) - (snapshots[i - 1] as f64)).max(0.0);
  535              ewma_delta = alpha * delta + (1.0 - alpha) * ewma_delta;
  536          }
  537          let Some(current) = snapshots.last().copied().map(|value| value as f64) else {
  538              return false;
  539          };
  540          let projected = current + ewma_delta * cfg.lookahead_turns as f64;
  541
  542          crate::logging::info(&format!(
  543              "[compaction/proactive] current={:.0} ewma_delta={:.1}/turn projected@{}turns={:.0} threshold={:.0}",
  544              current, ewma_delta, cfg.lookahead_turns, projected, threshold
  545          ));
  546
  547          projected >= threshold
  548      }
  549
  550      // ── Semantic mode trigger ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  551
  552      /// Returns `true` if the semantic strategy detects a topic shift or
  553      /// predicts we should compact now.
  554      ///
  555      /// Topic-shift detection: compares the mean embedding of the oldest half
  556      /// of the history window against the newest half. A low cosine similarity
  557      /// between the two clusters indicates a topic boundary was crossed —
  558      /// the previous topic is complete and safe to summarize.
  559      ///
  560      /// Falls back to proactive logic if embeddings are unavailable.
  561      fn should_compact_semantic(&self, all_messages: &[Message]) -> bool {
  562          if self.anti_signals_block(all_messages) {
  563              return false;
  564          }
  565
  566          // Need enough embedding history to split into two halves.
  567          let history_len = self.embedding_history.len();
  568          if history_len < 4 {
  569              // Fall back to proactive trigger.
  570              return self.should_compact_proactively(all_messages);
  571          }
  572
  573          let cfg = &self.compaction_config;
  574          let half = history_len / 2;
  575
  576          let old_embeddings: Vec<&Vec<f32>> = self.embedding_history.iter().take(half).collect();
  577          let new_embeddings: Vec<&Vec<f32>> = self.embedding_history.iter().skip(half).collect();
  578
  579          let dim = old_embeddings[0].len();
  580
  581          // Compute mean embedding for each half.
  582          let mean_old = mean_embedding(&old_embeddings, dim);
  583          let mean_new = mean_embedding(&new_embeddings, dim);
  584
  585          let similarity = crate::embedding::cosine_similarity(&mean_old, &mean_new);
  586
  587          crate::logging::info(&format!(
  588              "[compaction/semantic] topic similarity (old vs new half) = {:.3} (threshold={:.2})",
  589              similarity, cfg.topic_shift_threshold
  590          ));
  591
  592          if similarity < cfg.topic_shift_threshold {
  593              crate::logging::info(
  594                  "[compaction/semantic] Topic shift detected — triggering proactive compaction",
  595              );
  596              return true;
  597          }
  598
  599          // No topic shift — still fall back to proactive growth check.
  600          self.should_compact_proactively(all_messages)
  601      }
  602
  603      /// Build a relevance-scored keep set for semantic compaction.
  604      ///
  605      /// Embeds the last `goal_window_turns` messages to represent the current
  606      /// goal, then scores all active messages by cosine similarity. Returns the
  607      /// cutoff index: messages before the cutoff will be summarized, messages at
  608      /// or after are kept verbatim.
  609      ///
  610      /// Messages above `relevance_keep_threshold` anywhere in the history are
  611      /// pulled out of the summarize set. Falls back to the standard recency
  612      /// cutoff if embeddings fail.
  613      fn semantic_cutoff(&mut self, active: &[Message]) -> usize {
  614          let goal_window_turns = self.compaction_config.goal_window_turns;

07
L1factmodelContext

07 · 上下文溢出与请求体过大走不同紧急恢复路径

源码事实

context-limit error 触发同步 hard compact 并重置 cache/tool/provider session；HTTP 413 类 byte-size 错误先从持久历史剥离超大 inline images；超长 OpenAI encrypted compaction 则丢 native state 改文本 fallback。

白话解释

“字太多”“图片字节太大”“服务端压缩包本身太大”不是同一种病，分别治疗。

对自研 Harness 的含义

避免对 413 做无效 token 摘要；紧急路径会改写持久 transcript，报告需明确这是有损恢复。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 90–135
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 136–182
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 119301ac9d60 · 打开账本提交 ↗
crates/jcode-app-core/src/agent/compaction.rs:90–182固定提交 ↗
   90      fn is_context_limit_error(error: &str) -> bool {
   91          let lower = error.to_lowercase();
   92          lower.contains("context length")
   93              || lower.contains("context window")
   94              || lower.contains("maximum context")
   95              || lower.contains("max context")
   96              || lower.contains("token limit")
   97              || lower.contains("too many tokens")
   98              || lower.contains("prompt is too long")
   99              || lower.contains("input is too long")
  100              || lower.contains("request too large")
  101              || lower.contains("length limit")
  102              || lower.contains("maximum tokens")
  103              || (lower.contains("exceeded") && lower.contains("tokens"))
  104      }
  105
  106      /// Best-effort emergency recovery after a context-limit error.
  107      ///
  108      /// Performs a synchronous hard compaction and resets provider session state,
  109      /// allowing the caller to retry the same turn immediately.
  110      pub(super) fn try_auto_compact_after_context_limit(&mut self, error: &str) -> bool {
  111          if crate::provider::openai_request::is_openai_encrypted_content_too_large_error(error)
  112              && self.try_recover_oversized_openai_native_compaction()
  113          {
  114              return true;
  115          }
  116          // A provider HTTP 413 ("request too large") is a *byte-size* failure
  117          // driven by inline base64 images, not a token-context overflow. Token
  118          // accounting deliberately undercounts images, so ordinary compaction
  119          // would not shrink the payload and the retry would 413 again. Strip
  120          // oversized images first.
  121          if self.try_recover_after_payload_too_large(error) {
  122              return true;
  123          }
  124          if !Self::is_context_limit_error(error) {
  125              return false;
  126          }
  127          if !self.provider.supports_compaction() {
  128              return false;
  129          }
  130
  131          let context_limit = self.provider.context_window() as u64;
  132          let compaction = self.registry.compaction();
  133
  134          let (dropped, usage_pct) = match compaction.try_write() {
  135              Ok(mut manager) => {
  136                  let (dropped, usage_pct) = {
  137                      let all_messages = self.session.provider_messages();
  138                      manager.update_observed_input_tokens(context_limit);
  139                      let usage_pct = manager.context_usage_with(all_messages) * 100.0;
  140                      let dropped = match manager.hard_compact_with(all_messages) {
  141                          Ok(dropped) => dropped,
  142                          Err(reason) => {
  143                              logging::warn(&format!(
  144                                  "Context-limit auto-recovery failed: hard compact failed ({})",
  145                                  reason
  146                              ));
  147                              return false;
  148                          }
  149                      };
  150                      (dropped, usage_pct)
  151                  };
  152                  self.sync_session_compaction_state_from_manager(&manager);
  153                  (dropped, usage_pct)
  154              }
  155              Err(_) => {
  156                  logging::warn("Context-limit auto-recovery skipped: compaction manager lock busy");
  157                  return false;
  158              }
  159          };
  160
  161          self.cache_tracker.reset();
  162          self.locked_tools = None;
  163          self.provider_session_id = None;
  164          self.session.provider_session_id = None;
  165
  166          logging::warn(&format!(
  167              "Context limit exceeded; auto-compacted and retrying (dropped {} messages, usage was {:.1}%)",
  168              dropped, usage_pct
  169          ));
  170          crate::runtime_memory_log::emit_event(
  171              crate::runtime_memory_log::RuntimeMemoryLogEvent::new(
  172                  "auto_compaction_applied",
  173                  "context_limit_auto_compaction",
  174              )
  175              .with_session_id(self.session.id.clone())
  176              .with_detail(format!(
  177                  "dropped_messages={dropped},usage_pct={usage_pct:.1}"
  178              ))
  179              .force_attribution(),
  180          );
  181
  182          true
  183      }
  184
  185      /// Best-effort recovery after a provider HTTP 413 "request too large" error.
  186      ///
  187      /// This failure is caused by the serialized request body (dominated by inline
  188      /// base64 images) exceeding the provider's size cap, which is independent of
  189      /// the token context window. We strip oversized images from the persisted
  190      /// transcript, oldest-first, down to a conservative byte budget and reset the
  191      /// provider session/cache so the caller can retry the same turn immediately.
  192      fn try_recover_after_payload_too_large(&mut self, error: &str) -> bool {
  193          if !crate::compaction::is_request_payload_too_large_error(error) {
  194              return false;
  195          }
  196
  197          let stripped = self
  198              .session
  199              .strip_oversized_images(crate::compaction::PAYLOAD_IMAGE_CHAR_BUDGET);
  200          if stripped == 0 {
  201              logging::warn(
  202                  "Request-too-large recovery skipped: no oversized inline images to strip",
  203              );
  204              return false;
  205          }
  206
  207          // The transcript changed; reseed compaction bookkeeping and reset
  208          // provider session/cache state so the retry sends the reduced payload.
  209          let compaction = self.registry.compaction();
  210          if let Ok(mut manager) = compaction.try_write() {
  211              let provider_messages = self.session.messages_for_provider();
  212              manager.reset();
  213              manager.set_budget(self.provider.context_window());
  214              if let Some(state) = self.session.compaction.as_ref() {
  215                  manager.restore_persisted_state_with(state, &provider_messages);
  216              } else {
  217                  manager.seed_restored_messages_with(&provider_messages);
  218              }
  219              self.sync_session_compaction_state_from_manager(&manager);
  220          }
  221
  222          self.cache_tracker.reset();
  223          self.locked_tools = None;
  224          self.provider_session_id = None;
  225          self.session.provider_session_id = None;
  226
  227          logging::warn(&format!(
  228              "Request body exceeded provider size limit; stripped {} oversized inline image(s) and retrying",
  229              stripped
  230          ));
  231          crate::runtime_memory_log::emit_event(
  232              crate::runtime_memory_log::RuntimeMemoryLogEvent::new(
  233                  "payload_too_large_recovered",
  234                  "request_payload_too_large",
  235              )
  236              .with_session_id(self.session.id.clone())
  237              .with_detail(format!("images_stripped={stripped}"))
  238              .force_attribution(),
  239          );
  240
  241          true
  242      }
  243
  244      fn try_recover_oversized_openai_native_compaction(&mut self) -> bool {
  245          let compaction = self.registry.compaction();
  246          let recovered = match compaction.try_write() {
  247              Ok(mut manager) => {
  248                  if !manager.discard_oversized_openai_native_compaction() {
  249                      return false;
  250                  }
  251                  self.sync_session_compaction_state_from_manager(&manager);
  252                  true
  253              }

08
L1factmodelContext

08 · 跨会话 memory 是独立后台 Agent，不阻塞主 turn

源码事实

MemoryAgent 通过容量 16 的 channel 接收主 Agent context，使用 embedding 检索、Haiku/sidecar relevance judge 和 memory-only tools；结果放入 pending memory，下一次新 user turn 才消费。

白话解释

主 Agent 回答时，旁边有个只管记忆的小秘书；它来不及的结果不会卡住当前回答，而是下一轮再递纸条。

对自研 Harness 的含义

延迟低且职责隔离，但 memory freshness 天生滞后一轮，channel 满时 try_send 可能丢更新。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–22
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 23–45
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

    1  //! Persistent Memory Agent
    2  //!
    3  //! A dedicated Haiku-powered agent for memory management that runs alongside
    4  //! the main agent. It has access to memory-specific tools only (no code execution).
    5  //!
    6  //! Architecture:
    7  //! - Receives context updates from main agent via channel
    8  //! - Uses embeddings for fast similarity search
    9  //! - Uses Haiku LLM to decide what's relevant and dig deeper
   10  //! - Surfaces relevant memories to main agent via PENDING_MEMORY
   11

   27  /// Context from a retrieval operation for post-retrieval maintenance
   28  #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
   29  struct RetrievalContext {
   30      /// Memory IDs that were verified as relevant by Haiku
   31      verified_ids: Vec<String>,
   32      /// Memory IDs that were retrieved but rejected by Haiku
   33      rejected_ids: Vec<String>,
   34      /// Brief snippet of the context for gap logging
   35      context_snippet: String,
   36  }
   37
   38  /// Channel capacity for context updates
   39  const CONTEXT_CHANNEL_CAPACITY: usize = 16;
   40
   41  /// Similarity threshold for topic change detection (lower = more different)
   42  const TOPIC_CHANGE_THRESHOLD: f32 = 0.3;
   43
   44  /// Maximum memories to surface per turn
   45  const MAX_MEMORIES_PER_TURN: usize = 5;
   46
   47  /// Dynamic no-sidecar gate tunables (variable-k surfacing without an LLM).
   48  ///
   49  /// When the memory sidecar is disabled (no LLM to judge relevance), we used to
   50  /// blindly pad the hybrid top-5 every turn, which injected ~5 memories even on
   51  /// turns that needed none. Instead we keep a score-relative window: always keep
   52  /// the top candidate, then keep each following candidate only while its hybrid
   53  /// score stays within `GATE_REL_FLOOR` of the top AND within `GATE_DROP_RATIO`
   54  /// of the previous kept score. The first big gap cuts the tail. This injects a
   55  /// VARIABLE count (1..=MAX_MEMORIES_PER_TURN) instead of a fixed 5.
   56  ///
   57  /// Bench (self-dev corpus, 150 query windows): precision@5 0.23 -> 0.36 (+56%),
   58  /// avg injected 5.0 -> ~2.25/turn, at zero added cost. Note this cannot drop to
   59  /// 0 on no-memory turns (cosdiag proved no zero-cost score separates them); the
   60  /// only lever for true 0-injection is the LLM precision rerank (sidecar mode).
   61  const GATE_REL_FLOOR: f32 = 0.90;
   62  const GATE_DROP_RATIO: f32 = 0.95;
   63
   64  /// Score-relative dynamic gate over hybrid-ranked `(entry, score)` candidates.
   65  ///
   66  /// Always keeps the top candidate, then keeps each following candidate only
   67  /// while its score stays within `GATE_REL_FLOOR` of the top score AND within
   68  /// `GATE_DROP_RATIO` of the previously kept score; the first gap that breaks
   69  /// either bound truncates the tail. Caps output at `max_k`. Returns a variable
   70  /// count (1..=max_k for a non-empty input), not a fixed top-k.
   71  fn dynamic_gate_select(
   72      candidates: Vec<(MemoryEntry, f32)>,
   73      max_k: usize,
   74  ) -> Vec<(MemoryEntry, f32)> {
   75      if candidates.is_empty() {
   76          return Vec::new();
   77      }
   78      let top = candidates[0].1.max(f32::MIN_POSITIVE);
   79      let mut prev = top;
   80      let mut out: Vec<(MemoryEntry, f32)> = Vec::new();
   81      for (entry, sim) in candidates.into_iter().take(max_k) {
   82          if !out.is_empty() && (sim < top * GATE_REL_FLOOR || sim < prev * GATE_DROP_RATIO) {
   83              break;
   84          }
   85          prev = sim;
   86          out.push((entry, sim));
   87      }
   88      out
   89  }
   90
   91  /// Reset surfaced memories every N turns to allow re-surfacing
   92  const TURN_RESET_INTERVAL: usize = 50;
   93
   94  /// How often to run periodic cluster refinement in post-retrieval maintenance.
   95  const CLUSTER_REFINEMENT_INTERVAL: u64 = 50;
   96

   98  static MEMORY_AGENT: tokio::sync::OnceCell<MemoryAgentHandle> = tokio::sync::OnceCell::const_new();
   99  static MAINTENANCE_TICK: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);

  116  });

09
L1factmodelContext

09 · memory 分 project/global，带向量空间标识与重复抑制

源码事实

MemoryManager 按 working directory hash 存 project graph，也支持 global；embedding 写入 model id，避免跨向量空间比较。pending 注入按 prompt signature、memory set overlap 和 TTL 抑制重复，并把已从本会话抽取的 memory 标为 known。

白话解释

不同项目各有笔记本；换了 embedding 模型不会把两种坐标硬比。刚提过的记忆也不会每轮重复唠叨。

对自研 Harness 的含义

比单纯向量 top-k 多了去重与 provenance；路径 hash 迁移/重命名会形成新的 project memory scope。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 159–171
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 172–185
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  159  trait MemoryEntryEmbeddingExt {
  160      fn ensure_embedding(&mut self) -> bool;

  162
  163  impl MemoryEntryEmbeddingExt for MemoryEntry {
  164      /// Generate and set embedding if not already present.
  165      /// Returns true if embedding was generated, false if already exists or failed.
  166      fn ensure_embedding(&mut self) -> bool {
  167          if self.embedding.is_some() {
  168              return false;
  169          }
  170
  171          match crate::embedding_backend::embed_passage_active(&self.content) {
  172              Ok((embedding, model_id)) => {
  173                  // Tag with the ACTIVE backend's model id so dense search only
  174                  // compares vectors from the same model/vector space. Untagged
  175                  // legacy memories are treated as local MiniLM via
  176                  // effective_embedding_model().
  177                  self.set_embedding(Some(embedding), Some(model_id));
  178                  true
  179              }
  180              Err(err) => {
  181                  crate::logging::info(&format!("Failed to generate embedding: {err}"));
  182                  false
  183              }
  184          }
  185      }
  186  }

05
DIMENSION · TOOLS

工具注册与执行

3 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1facttools

01 · 工具是 typed registry，定义顺序与 intent 字段集中标准化

源码事实

Tool trait 统一 name/description/JSON Schema/async execute；to_definition 集中给所有 object schema（含 MCP proxy）加入必填 intent。Registry 内建读写编辑、shell、浏览器、web、swarm、memory、goal、gmail、schedule、自开发等工具，并按名称排序 definitions。

白话解释

每件工具都交同一种说明书和回执；“为什么调用”不是每个工具自己记得就写，而是总装配线强制补上。

对自研 Harness 的含义

便于 UI 显示与审计，稳定排序提高 prompt cache hit。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 9–36
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 37–65
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   14  pub fn intent_schema_property() -> Value {
   15      serde_json::json!({
   16          "type": "string",
   17          "description": TOOL_INTENT_DESCRIPTION,
   18      })
   19  }
   20
   21  /// Ensure a tool parameter schema declares the shared `intent` property and
   22  /// marks it required. Applied centrally when converting tools to provider
   23  /// definitions so every tool (including MCP proxies) asks the model for an
   24  /// intent without each tool wiring it manually.
   25  pub fn ensure_intent_in_schema(mut schema: Value) -> Value {
   26      let Some(object) = schema.as_object_mut() else {
   27          return schema;
   28      };
   29      // Only touch object-shaped parameter schemas.
   30      let is_object_schema = object
   31          .get("type")
   32          .and_then(|t| t.as_str())
   33          .map(|t| t == "object")
   34          .unwrap_or_else(|| object.contains_key("properties"));
   35      if !is_object_schema {
   36          return schema;
   37      }
   38
   39      let properties = object
   40          .entry("properties")
   41          .or_insert_with(|| Value::Object(serde_json::Map::new()));
   42      if let Some(properties) = properties.as_object_mut() {
   43          properties
   44              .entry("intent")
   45              .or_insert_with(intent_schema_property);
   46      } else {
   47          return schema;
   48      }
   49
   50      match object.get_mut("required") {
   51          Some(Value::Array(required)) => {
   52              if !required.iter().any(|v| v.as_str() == Some("intent")) {
   53                  required.push(Value::String("intent".to_string()));
   54              }
   55          }
   56          _ => {
   57              object.insert(
   58                  "required".to_string(),
   59                  Value::Array(vec![Value::String("intent".to_string())]),
   60              );
   61          }
   62      }
   63
   64      schema
   65  }

    9  pub const TOOL_INTENT_DESCRIPTION: &str =
   10      "Required short label shown in the UI: why this call is being made.";
   11
   12  /// Input key a caller sets to accept the token cost of an oversized result.
   13  ///
   14  /// The context guard withholds any tool result too large for the remaining
   15  /// context and states its token cost. Setting this repeats the call and spends
   16  /// that cost deliberately. Kept in sync with the registry constant of the same
   17  /// name, which reads the flag off raw tool input.
   18  pub const ACCEPT_LARGE_OUTPUT_KEY: &str = "accept_large_output";
   19
   20  /// Deliberately terse: this rides on every tool schema on every request, so
   21  /// each word is paid forever. The full explanation lives in the refusal
   22  /// message, which is only ever shown when it is actually relevant.
   23  pub const ACCEPT_LARGE_OUTPUT_DESCRIPTION: &str =
   24      "Re-run accepting the stated token cost of a withheld result.";
   25
   26  pub fn intent_schema_property() -> Value {
   27      serde_json::json!({
   28          "type": "string",
   29          "description": TOOL_INTENT_DESCRIPTION,
   30      })
   31  }
   32
   33  pub fn accept_large_output_schema_property() -> Value {
   34      serde_json::json!({
   35          "type": "boolean",
   36          "description": ACCEPT_LARGE_OUTPUT_DESCRIPTION,
   37      })
   38  }
   39
   40  /// Ensure a tool parameter schema declares the shared `intent` property and
   41  /// marks it required. Applied centrally when converting tools to provider
   42  /// definitions so every tool (including MCP proxies) asks the model for an
   43  /// intent without each tool wiring it manually.
   44  ///
   45  /// The optional `accept_large_output` escape hatch is added the same way. Any
   46  /// tool can produce a result too large to return, so documenting it per tool
   47  /// would mean editing dozens of schemas and missing MCP proxies entirely.
   48  pub fn ensure_intent_in_schema(mut schema: Value) -> Value {
   49      let Some(object) = schema.as_object_mut() else {
   50          return schema;
   51      };
   52      // Only touch object-shaped parameter schemas.
   53      let is_object_schema = object
   54          .get("type")
   55          .and_then(|t| t.as_str())
   56          .map(|t| t == "object")
   57          .unwrap_or_else(|| object.contains_key("properties"));
   58      if !is_object_schema {
   59          return schema;
   60      }
   61
   62      let properties = object
   63          .entry("properties")
   64          .or_insert_with(|| Value::Object(serde_json::Map::new()));
   65      if let Some(properties) = properties.as_object_mut() {
   66          properties
   67              .entry("intent")
   68              .or_insert_with(intent_schema_property);
   69          // Optional, so it is deliberately not added to `required`.
   70          properties
   71              .entry(ACCEPT_LARGE_OUTPUT_KEY)
   72              .or_insert_with(accept_large_output_schema_property);
   73      } else {
   74          return schema;
   75      }
   76
   77      match object.get_mut("required") {
   78          Some(Value::Array(required)) => {
   79              if !required.iter().any(|v| v.as_str() == Some("intent")) {
   80                  required.push(Value::String("intent".to_string()));
   81              }
   82          }
   83          _ => {
   84              object.insert(
   85                  "required".to_string(),
   86                  Value::Array(vec![Value::String("intent".to_string())]),
   87              );
   88          }
   89      }
   90
   91      schema
   92  }

02
L1facttools

02 · 普通模型工具调用串行，显式 batch 最多并发十个

源码事实

run_turn 对 tool_calls 逐个 await registry.execute 并逐条持久化结果；BatchTool 禁止递归 batch，最多 10 个 subcalls，用 FuturesUnordered 并发执行、实时发布 progress，最终恢复原调用顺序。

白话解释

模型一次开几张普通工单时按顺序做；只有明确使用 batch 托盘，才会同时跑最多十件事。

对自研 Harness 的含义

默认副作用顺序可预测；性能并行需要模型主动选择 batch。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 878–896
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 897–917
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

03
L1facttools

03 · 工具输出有双预算闸门，前台命令超时可升级后台

源码事实

Registry 在输出将超过 context 90% 或单个输出超过 budget 30% 时截断。Bash 默认前台 timeout 120s、上限 10 分钟；前台超时不会杀进程，而是把 JoinHandle 交给 background manager。显式后台模式按 timeout 杀整个 Unix process group 并写 output/status file。

白话解释

一份巨型日志不能吃掉整本上下文；长命令如果只是前台等不及，会转到后台继续，而显式后台设置的超时才负责杀进程组。

对自研 Harness 的含义

特别适合长 build/test；用户必须理解前台 timeout 是“停止等待”而不是“停止执行”。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 627–651
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 652–678
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  642
  643      /// Check if a tool output would overflow the context window and truncate if needed.
  644      /// Returns the (possibly truncated) output.
  645      async fn guard_context_overflow(&self, tool_name: &str, output: ToolOutput) -> ToolOutput {
  646          let compaction = self.compaction.read().await;
  647          let budget = compaction.token_budget();
  648          if budget == 0 {
  649              return output;
  650          }
  651
  652          let current_tokens = compaction.effective_token_count();
  653          let output_tokens = Self::estimate_tokens(&output.output);
  654
  655          // Check 1: Would adding this output push us over the safety threshold?
  656          let projected = current_tokens + output_tokens;
  657          let threshold_tokens = (budget as f32 * Self::CONTEXT_GUARD_THRESHOLD) as usize;
  658
  659          // Check 2: Is this single output unreasonably large relative to budget?
  660          let single_max_tokens = (budget as f32 * Self::SINGLE_OUTPUT_MAX_FRACTION) as usize;
  661
  662          let needs_truncation = projected > threshold_tokens || output_tokens > single_max_tokens;
  663
  664          if !needs_truncation {
  665              return output;
  666          }
  667
  668          // Calculate how many tokens we can afford for this output
  669          let remaining = if current_tokens < threshold_tokens {
  670              threshold_tokens - current_tokens
  671          } else {
  672              // Already over threshold — allow a small amount for the error message
  673              budget / 50 // ~2% of budget for the truncation notice
  674          };
  675          let max_tokens = remaining.min(single_max_tokens);
  676
  677          // Convert token limit back to approximate character limit
  678          let max_chars = max_tokens * 4;
  679
  680          if output.output.len() <= max_chars {
  681              return output;
  682          }
  683
  684          crate::logging::info(&format!(
  685              "Context guard: truncating {} output from ~{}k to ~{}k tokens \
  686               (context: {}k/{}k, {:.0}% used)",
  687              tool_name,
  688              output_tokens / 1000,
  689              max_tokens / 1000,
  690              current_tokens / 1000,
  691              budget / 1000,
  692              (current_tokens as f32 / budget as f32) * 100.0,
  693          ));
  694
  695          // Truncate the output, keeping the beginning (usually most relevant)
  696          let truncated = if max_chars > 200 {
  697              // Keep beginning of output + truncation notice
  698              let kept = &output.output[..output.output.floor_char_boundary(max_chars - 150)];
  699              format!(
  700                  "{}\n\n⚠️ OUTPUT TRUNCATED: This tool output was {:.0}k tokens which would \
  701                   exceed the context window ({:.0}k/{}k tokens used, {}k budget). \
  702                   Only the first ~{:.0}k tokens are shown. Use more targeted queries \
  703                   (e.g., smaller line ranges, specific grep patterns) to get the content \
  704                   you need without exceeding context limits.",
  705                  kept,
  706                  output_tokens as f32 / 1000.0,
  707                  current_tokens as f32 / 1000.0,
  708                  budget / 1000,
  709                  budget / 1000,
  710                  max_tokens as f32 / 1000.0,
  711              )
  712          } else {
  713              // Context is almost completely full — just return error
  714              format!(
  715                  "⚠️ CONTEXT LIMIT REACHED: Cannot return this tool output (~{:.0}k tokens) \
  716                   because the context window is nearly full ({:.0}k/{}k tokens). \
  717                   Consider using /compact to free up space, or use more targeted queries.",
  718                  output_tokens as f32 / 1000.0,
  719                  current_tokens as f32 / 1000.0,
  720                  budget / 1000,
  721              )
  722          };
  723
  724          ToolOutput {
  725              output: truncated,
  726              title: output.title,
  727              metadata: output.metadata,
  728              images: output.images,
  729          }
  730      }
  731

  637      /// Execute a tool by name
  638      pub async fn execute(&self, name: &str, input: Value, ctx: ToolContext) -> Result<ToolOutput> {
  639          // Mark this call in-flight for the whole execution so the missing
  640          // tool-output repair paths do not mistake a slow tool for an
  641          // interrupted one and inject a duplicate synthetic result. See
  642          // `tool::inflight`.
  643          let _in_flight = inflight::mark_tool_in_flight(&ctx.tool_call_id);
  644          let tools = self.tools.read().await;
  645          let resolved_name = Self::resolve_tool_name(name);
  646          if let Some(policy) = session_tool_policy(&ctx.session_id) {
  647              if let Some(allowed) = policy.allowed_tools.as_ref()
  648                  && !tool_name_is_allowed(allowed, resolved_name)
  649              {
  650                  return Err(anyhow::anyhow!("Tool '{}' is not allowed", resolved_name));
  651              }
  652              if tool_name_is_disabled(&policy.disabled_tools, resolved_name) {
  653                  return Err(anyhow::anyhow!("Tool '{}' is disabled", resolved_name));
  654              }
  655          }
  656          let tool = match tools.get(resolved_name) {
  657              Some(tool) => tool.clone(),
  658              None => {
  659                  // List available tools so the model can recover instead of
  660                  // spiraling through hallucinated names like "ToolSearch" (#104).
  661                  let mut available: Vec<&str> = tools.keys().map(|k| k.as_str()).collect();
  662                  available.sort_unstable();
  663                  let suggestions = Self::closest_tool_names(name, &available);
  664                  let mut msg = format!("Unknown tool: {name}.");
  665                  if !suggestions.is_empty() {
  666                      msg.push_str(&format!(" Did you mean: {}?", suggestions.join(", ")));
  667                  }
  668                  msg.push_str(&format!(" Available tools: {}.", available.join(", ")));
  669                  return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(msg));
  670              }
  671          };
  672
  673          // Drop the lock before executing
  674          drop(tools);
  675
  676          // User-configured pre_tool gate: external policy hook that can block
  677          // this call (exit 2). Skipped entirely when not configured.
  678          if crate::hooks::hook_configured("pre_tool") {
  679              let input_json = input.to_string();
  680              let working_dir = ctx
  681                  .working_dir
  682                  .as_ref()
  683                  .map(|dir| dir.display().to_string());
  684              let decision = crate::hooks::run_pre_tool_gate(
  685                  &ctx.session_id,
  686                  working_dir.as_deref(),
  687                  resolved_name,
  688                  &input_json,
  689              )
  690              .await;
  691              if let crate::hooks::GateDecision::Block { reason } = decision {
  692                  let mut fields =
  693                      Self::tool_lifecycle_fields("blocked", name, resolved_name, &input, &ctx);
  694                  fields.push(("block_reason".to_string(), reason.clone()));
  695                  crate::logging::event_warn("TOOL_LIFECYCLE", fields);
  696                  return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
  697                      "Tool call blocked by pre_tool hook: {reason}"
  698                  ));
  699              }
  700          }
  701
  702          crate::logging::event_info(
  703              "TOOL_LIFECYCLE",
  704              Self::tool_lifecycle_fields("start", name, resolved_name, &input, &ctx),
  705          );
  706
  707          let started_at = std::time::Instant::now();
  708          let result = tool.execute(input.clone(), ctx.clone()).await;
  709          let latency_ms = started_at.elapsed().as_millis().min(u128::from(u64::MAX)) as u64;
  710
  711          crate::telemetry::record_tool_execution(resolved_name, &input, result.is_ok(), latency_ms);
  712          Self::fire_post_tool_hook(resolved_name, &ctx, &result, latency_ms);
  713
  714          let mut output = match result {
  715              Ok(output) => output,
  716              Err(error) => {
  717                  let mut fields =
  718                      Self::tool_lifecycle_fields("error", name, resolved_name, &input, &ctx);
  719                  fields.push(("elapsed_ms".to_string(), latency_ms.to_string()));
  720                  fields.push(("error".to_string(), crate::util::format_error_chain(&error)));
  721                  crate::logging::event_warn("TOOL_LIFECYCLE", fields);
  722                  return Err(error);
  723              }
  724          };
  725
  726          // Context overflow guard: check if this output would push us over the limit
  727          output = self
  728              .guard_context_overflow(name, output, accepts_large_output(&input))
  729              .await;
  730
  731          let mut fields = Self::tool_lifecycle_fields("done", name, resolved_name, &input, &ctx);
  732          fields.push(("elapsed_ms".to_string(), latency_ms.to_string()));
  733          fields.push(("output_bytes".to_string(), output.output.len().to_string()));
  734          fields.push((
  735              "output_chars".to_string(),
  736              output.output.chars().count().to_string(),
  737          ));
  738          fields.push(("image_count".to_string(), output.images.len().to_string()));
  739          crate::logging::event_info("TOOL_LIFECYCLE", fields);
  740
  741          Ok(output)
  742      }
  743
  744      /// Check if a tool output would overflow the context window and truncate if needed.
  745      /// Returns the (possibly truncated) output.
  746      ///
  747      /// An oversized result is **refused** rather than truncated. Truncating by
  748      /// default was the worse failure: the caller still paid the full remaining
  749      /// context for a prefix that usually did not contain the answer, and the
06
DIMENSION · CONNECTORS

连接器、插件与 MCP

2 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factconnectors

01 · MCP 区分共享池与 session-owned client

源码事实

daemon 中 shared:true 默认服务器复用 SharedMcpPool，shared:false 状态型服务器每 session 单独启动；并行 connect_all 不阻塞 agent spawn，第一次真实调用尚未连接的 server 时最多等待 30 秒。

白话解释

无状态工具服务像公共电梯，多会话共用；带浏览器状态的服务像独立房间，每个会话一套。

对自研 Harness 的含义

降低重复进程与连接成本，同时避免 stateful connector 串会话。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–29
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 30–59
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 119301ac9d60 · 打开账本提交 ↗
crates/jcode-base/src/mcp/manager.rs:1–59固定提交 ↗

    4  //! are managed by the pool and reused across sessions. Servers marked `shared: false`

   16  /// Bound on how long a tool call will wait for a not-yet-connected MCP server
   17  /// to come up before failing with a clean tool error. Keeps a slow/hanging
   18  /// server from blocking a single tool call forever (and never blocks spawn).
   19  const CONNECT_ON_CALL_TIMEOUT: std::time::Duration = std::time::Duration::from_secs(30);
   20
   21  /// Meter a completed tool call for partner-discovery provenance. No-op for
   22  /// servers without discovery provenance (the overwhelmingly common case) and
   23  /// whenever `sponsors.enabled` is false. Counts only; never content.
   24  fn meter_provenance_call(server: &str, result: &Result<ToolCallResult>) {
   25      let is_error = match result {
   26          Ok(res) => res.is_error,
   27          Err(_) => true,
   28      };
   29      crate::sponsors::provenance::on_tool_call(server, is_error);
   30  }

   46  /// In daemon mode, shared servers delegate to the SharedMcpPool while

  128              .config

  130              .iter()

    1  //! MCP Manager - manages MCP server connections for a single session.
    2  //!
    3  //! In daemon mode with a shared pool, servers marked `shared: true` (the default)
    4  //! are managed by the pool and reused across sessions. Servers marked `shared: false`
    5  //! (e.g., Playwright with browser state) are spawned per-session.
    6
    7  use super::client::{McpClient, McpHandle};
    8  use super::pool::SharedMcpPool;
    9  use super::protocol::{McpConfig, McpServerConfig, McpToolDef, ToolCallResult};
   10  use anyhow::{Context, Result};
   11  use serde::Serialize;
   12  use std::collections::HashMap;
   13  use std::sync::Arc;
   14  use tokio::sync::RwLock;
   15
   16  /// Bound on how long a tool call will wait for a not-yet-connected MCP server
   17  /// to come up before failing with a clean tool error. Keeps a slow/hanging
   18  /// server from blocking a single tool call forever (and never blocks spawn).
   19  const CONNECT_ON_CALL_TIMEOUT: std::time::Duration = std::time::Duration::from_secs(30);
   20
   21  /// Meter a completed tool call for partner-discovery provenance. No-op for
   22  /// servers without discovery provenance (the overwhelmingly common case) and
   23  /// whenever `sponsors.enabled` is false. Counts only; never content.
   24  fn meter_provenance_call(server: &str, result: &Result<ToolCallResult>) {
   25      let is_error = match result {
   26          Ok(res) => res.is_error,
   27          Err(_) => true,
   28      };
   29      crate::sponsors::provenance::on_tool_call(server, is_error);
   30  }

02
L1factconnectors

02 · MCP schema 晚到只允许一次 cache miss，JSON-RPC 请求按 ID 隔离

源码事实

首轮不等待慢 MCP；tool list 锁定后若发现新 mcp__ tools，仅重建一次 snapshot。McpHandle 用 atomic request id、pending oneshot map 与 30s request timeout，使共享 server 的并发响应互不串线。

白话解释

先让用户马上说话，不为插件启动卡住；插件工具后来到时只付一次缓存失效成本。多会话共用服务时每张工单都有号码。

对自研 Harness 的含义

启动延迟与 prompt cache 之间做了明确折中。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 335–363
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 364–393
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 119301ac9d60 · 打开账本提交 ↗
crates/jcode-app-core/src/agent/turn_execution.rs:335–393固定提交 ↗
  335      pub(super) async fn tool_definitions(&mut self) -> Vec<ToolDefinition> {
  336          if self.session.is_canary {
  337              self.registry.register_selfdev_tools().await;
  338          }
  339
  340          // Return locked tools if available (prevents cache invalidation from
  341          // tools arriving asynchronously after the first API request).
  342          //
  343          // Exception: MCP servers connect on a background task and register
  344          // `mcp__*` tools seconds after the session starts — typically *after*
  345          // the first turn has already locked the snapshot. We deliberately do
  346          // NOT block the first turn on MCP connection: servers can be slow or
  347          // hang, and we want the user to be able to talk to the agent the moment
  348          // the session spawns. The price is that the first locked snapshot is
  349          // missing MCP tools, and the only other unlock path fires when the model
  350          // calls the `mcp` management tool — which it cannot do without first
  351          // seeing MCP tools (#206).
  352          //
  353          // So, exactly once per locked snapshot, if MCP tools have since appeared
  354          // in the registry, we rebuild. This is a single intentional provider
  355          // prompt-cache miss (the turn MCP tools first appear). The
  356          // `mcp_late_register_resolved` flag makes this a one-shot check so we do
  357          // not rescan the registry on every subsequent turn.
  358          if let Some(ref locked) = self.locked_tools {
  359              if self.mcp_late_register_resolved {
  360                  return locked.clone();
  361              }
  362              if self.registry_has_new_mcp_tools(locked).await {
  363                  logging::info(
  364                      "MCP tools registered after first turn locked the tool snapshot — \
  365                       rebuilding once to expose them. This is one intentional prompt-cache \
  366                       miss; we accept it so the agent is reachable immediately at spawn \
  367                       instead of blocking on MCP connection (#206).",
  368                  );
  369                  // Latch the one-shot guard and drop the stale snapshot directly.
  370                  // We intentionally do NOT call `unlock_tools()` here, because that
  371                  // re-arms the guard (it is the explicit-reload path) and would let
  372                  // the recheck fire again on every later turn.
  373                  self.mcp_late_register_resolved = true;
  374                  self.locked_tools = None;
  375                  self.cache_tracker.reset();
  376              } else {
  377                  // No MCP tools have appeared. They may still be connecting, so
  378                  // leave the guard unset and re-check on the next turn. Once they
  379                  // appear (or never do, after the registry settles) we stop.
  380                  return locked.clone();
  381              }
  382          }
  383
  384          let tools = self.build_filtered_tool_definitions().await;
  385
  386          // Lock the tool list to prevent cache invalidation when more tools
  387          // arrive asynchronously mid-session.
  388          logging::info(&format!(
  389              "Locking tool list at {} tools for cache stability",
  390              tools.len()
  391          ));
  392          self.locked_tools = Some(tools.clone());
  393          tools
  394      }
  395
  396      /// Build the agent's tool definitions from the registry, applying the
  397      /// session's `allowed_tools`, `disabled_tools`, and self-dev filters.
  398      async fn build_filtered_tool_definitions(&self) -> Vec<ToolDefinition> {
  399          let mut tools = self.registry.definitions(self.allowed_tools.as_ref()).await;
  400          if !self.disabled_tools.is_empty() {
  401              tools.retain(|tool| !self.disabled_tools.contains(&tool.name));
  402          }
  403          Self::apply_selfdev_tool_surface(&mut tools, self.session.is_canary);
  404          tools
  405      }
  406
  407      /// Tailor the `selfdev` tool definition to the session mode.
  408      ///
  409      /// The registry stores a single shared `selfdev` tool with a default
  410      /// (non-self-dev) schema. Self-dev sessions get the full build/test/reload
  411      /// surface; every other session keeps the lightweight on-ramp surface
  412      /// (`enter`, `setup`, `reload`, `status`, `find-config`). The tool stays
  413      /// available in all sessions so the agent can always enter self-dev mode.
  414      fn apply_selfdev_tool_surface(tools: &mut [ToolDefinition], is_canary: bool) {
  415          for tool in tools.iter_mut() {
  416              if tool.name == "selfdev" {
  417                  tool.description =
  418                      crate::tool::selfdev::SelfDevTool::description_for(is_canary).to_string();
  419                  tool.input_schema = crate::tool::selfdev::SelfDevTool::schema_for(is_canary);
  420              }
  421          }
  422      }
  423
  424      /// Returns true if the registry contains `mcp__*` tools (subject to the
  425      /// session's `allowed_tools` filter) that are not present in the currently
  426      /// locked snapshot. Used to detect the async MCP-registration race (#206).
  427      async fn registry_has_new_mcp_tools(&self, locked: &[ToolDefinition]) -> bool {
  428          let registry_names = self.registry.tool_names().await;
  429          let allowed = self.allowed_tools.as_ref();
  430          registry_names.iter().any(|name| {
  431              name.starts_with("mcp__")
  432                  && allowed.map(|set| set.contains(name)).unwrap_or(true)
  433                  && !self.disabled_tools.contains(name)
  434                  && !locked.iter().any(|t| &t.name == name)
  435          })
  436      }
  437
  438      pub async fn tool_names(&self) -> Vec<String> {
  439          self.tool_definitions_for_debug()
  440              .await
  441              .into_iter()
  442              .map(|tool| tool.name)
  443              .collect()
  444      }
  445
  446      /// Get full tool definitions for debug introspection (bypasses lock)
  447      pub async fn tool_definitions_for_debug(&self) -> Vec<crate::message::ToolDefinition> {
  448          if self.session.is_canary {
  449              self.registry.register_selfdev_tools().await;
  450          }
  451          let mut tools = self.registry.definitions(self.allowed_tools.as_ref()).await;
  452          if !self.disabled_tools.is_empty() {
  453              tools.retain(|tool| !self.disabled_tools.contains(&tool.name));
  454          }
  455          Self::apply_selfdev_tool_surface(&mut tools, self.session.is_canary);
  456          tools
  457      }

  460          &self,
  461          name: &str,
  462          input: serde_json::Value,
  463      ) -> Result<crate::tool::ToolOutput> {
  464          self.validate_tool_allowed(name)?;

  381      pub(super) async fn tool_definitions(&mut self) -> Vec<ToolDefinition> {
  382          if self.session.is_canary {
  383              self.registry.register_selfdev_tools().await;
  384          }
  385
  386          // Return locked tools if available (prevents cache invalidation from
  387          // tools arriving asynchronously after the first API request).
  388          //
  389          // Exception: MCP servers connect on a background task and register
  390          // `mcp__*` tools seconds after the session starts — typically *after*
  391          // the first turn has already locked the snapshot. We deliberately do
  392          // NOT block the first turn on MCP connection: servers can be slow or
  393          // hang, and we want the user to be able to talk to the agent the moment
  394          // the session spawns. The price is that the first locked snapshot is
  395          // missing MCP tools, and the only other unlock path fires when the model
  396          // calls the `mcp` management tool — which it cannot do without first
  397          // seeing MCP tools (#206).
  398          //
  399          // So, exactly once per locked snapshot, if MCP tools have since appeared
  400          // in the registry, we rebuild. This is a single intentional provider
  401          // prompt-cache miss (the turn MCP tools first appear). The
  402          // `mcp_late_register_resolved` flag makes this a one-shot check so we do
  403          // not rescan the registry on every subsequent turn.
  404          if let Some(ref locked) = self.locked_tools {
  405              if self.mcp_late_register_resolved {
  406                  return locked.clone();
  407              }
  408              if self.registry_has_new_mcp_tools(locked).await {
  409                  logging::info(
  410                      "MCP tools registered after first turn locked the tool snapshot — \
  411                       rebuilding once to expose them. This is one intentional prompt-cache \
  412                       miss; we accept it so the agent is reachable immediately at spawn \
  413                       instead of blocking on MCP connection (#206).",
  414                  );
  415                  // Latch the one-shot guard and drop the stale snapshot directly.
  416                  // We intentionally do NOT call `unlock_tools()` here, because that
  417                  // re-arms the guard (it is the explicit-reload path) and would let
  418                  // the recheck fire again on every later turn.
  419                  self.mcp_late_register_resolved = true;
  420                  self.locked_tools = None;
  421                  self.cache_tracker.reset();
  422              } else {
  423                  // No MCP tools have appeared. They may still be connecting, so
  424                  // leave the guard unset and re-check on the next turn. Once they
  425                  // appear (or never do, after the registry settles) we stop.
  426                  return locked.clone();
  427              }
  428          }
  429
  430          let tools = self.build_filtered_tool_definitions().await;
  431
  432          // Lock the tool list to prevent cache invalidation when more tools
  433          // arrive asynchronously mid-session.
  434          logging::info(&format!(
  435              "Locking tool list at {} tools for cache stability",
  436              tools.len()
  437          ));
  438          self.locked_tools = Some(tools.clone());
  439          tools
  440      }
  441
  442      /// Build the agent's tool definitions from the registry, applying the
  443      /// session's `allowed_tools`, `disabled_tools`, and self-dev filters.
  444      async fn build_filtered_tool_definitions(&self) -> Vec<ToolDefinition> {
  445          let mut tools = self.registry.definitions(self.allowed_tools.as_ref()).await;
  446          if !self.disabled_tools.is_empty() {
  447              tools.retain(|tool| {
  448                  !crate::tool::tool_name_is_disabled(&self.disabled_tools, &tool.name)
  449              });
  450          }
  451          Self::apply_selfdev_tool_surface(&mut tools, self.session.is_canary);
  452          tools
  453      }
  454
  455      /// Expose the `selfdev` tool only while running in self-development mode.
  456      ///
  457      /// The registry keeps the implementation available for self-dev sessions,
  458      /// but regular agents should not spend tool-list context on an internal
  459      /// development surface.
  460      fn apply_selfdev_tool_surface(tools: &mut Vec<ToolDefinition>, is_canary: bool) {
  461          if !is_canary {
  462              tools.retain(|tool| tool.name != "selfdev");
  463              return;
  464          }
07
DIMENSION · SECURITY

安全、审批与权限

3 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factsecurity

01 · 工具边界由 session allow/disable 与外部 pre_tool gate 双层控制

源码事实

Registry.execute 先解析 alias，再检查 session allowed_tools/disabled_tools；若配置 pre_tool hook，退出 2 阻止调用并把 stderr 反馈模型，其他非零、超时或启动失败均 fail-open。

白话解释

先看这把工具是否在本会话工具箱里，再给企业自定义门卫一次否决机会；门卫自己坏了时默认放行。

对自研 Harness 的含义

可扩展治理强，但 pre_tool 的 fail-open 语义不适合要求故障关闭的高风险环境。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 543–571
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 572–601
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。
